The resulting large-scale mixed-integer nonlinear program admits a set partitioning reformulation whose column coefficients are not available in closed form. We therefore develop a Branch-Price-and-Evaluation framework for set partitioning problems with externally computable column coefficients: upper-bounding surrogates guide pricing, and iterative exact evaluation updates the coefficients of active columns. Computational results show that explicit energy modeling significantly reduces false service promises and yields more credible planning decisions. They also reveal a load-dependent reversal in zoning: pooling is preferable under light demand, whereas tighter zoning becomes more profitable as demand increases. Over the tested range, profitability is driven more by zoning than by battery improvement, suggesting that managers should get service zone design right before investing in battery upgrades; this caution is reinforced by the counterintuitive finding that larger batteries may delay replenishment and reduce fleet readiness under sparse demand. These findings show that energy feasibility is not merely a matter of battery-capacity expansion, but a design dimension that shapes service-zone configuration.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [The Role of Input Dimensionality in the Emergence and Targeted Control of Adversarial Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26207)
*Nasrin Malekzadeh Goradel,Niccolo Pancino,Yaser Gholizade Atani,Benedetta Tondi,Giovanni Bellettini,Mauro Barni*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究揭示了输入维度是引发对抗样本的关键因素，并证明在高维空间中，定向与非定向攻击的难度差距会随维度上升而显著缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释深度神经网络对抗脆弱性的高维几何理论缺乏系统的实证支撑，且相关假设未得到充分验证。

Method: 对现有基于测度集中（concentration of measure）的理论框架进行分析，并对不同输入维度、不同层级的数据集及多种神经网络架构进行了大规模的实证评估。

Result: 1. 现实图像类表现出比现有理论假设更强的经验局部化；2. 随着输入维度增加，生成对抗样本变得更容易；3. 高维空间中，强制模型输出特定目标标签（定向攻击）相比非定向攻击仅需有限的额外扰动，且该差距随维度增加而减小。

Conclusion: 输入维度是导致对抗样本出现及其定向攻击控制的核心因素。高维几何意味着定向攻击与非定向攻击之间的扰动差距会随维度增加而缩小，但该现象的根源是几何属性与数据分布的交互，还是神经网络架构的特性，仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: Several theoretical works have tried to explain the adversarial vulnerability of deep neural networks through properties of high-dimensional geometry. However, the assumptions underlying these works are rarely examined empirically, and systematic evidence remains limited. In this work, we present a systematic study of the role of input dimensionality in both the emergence and the targeted control of adversarial examples. We first analyse the scope and limitations of existing theoretical frameworks based on concentration of measure, showing that real image classes exhibit strong empirical localization, beyond what such theories typically assume. We then conduct an extensive empirical evaluation across hierarchical image datasets spanning a wide range of input dimensionalities and diverse neural architectures. Our results consistently show that adversarial examples become easier to construct as dimensionality increases. We also investigate how input dimensionality affects the additional difficulty of crafting targeted adversarial examples. In particular, we provide theoretical arguments showing that high-dimensional geometry implies that enforcing a specific target label entails only a limited additional distortion compared to untargeted attacks. We corroborate this insight through extensive experiments, demonstrating that the gap between targeted and untargeted perturbations remains small and further narrows as input dimensionality increases. While, taken together, our findings establish high input dimensionality as a fundamental factor underlying the emergence and targeted control of adversarial examples, whether this phenomenon primarily arises from the interplay between high-dimensional geometry and data distributions or from the architectural properties of deep neural networks remains an open question.

</details>


### [180] [A probabilistic framework for online test-time adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26457)
*Daniel Corrales,David Ríos Insua*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于状态空间模型的概率框架，通过对参数随时间演化的建模，实现了模型在分布偏移场景下的在线测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决模型在训练分布与测试分布存在分布偏移（Distributional Shift）的情况下，如何仅利用无标签测试数据进行在线测试时自适应（Online Test-Time Adaptation）的难题。

Method: 提出了一种基于状态空间模型（State-Space Modeling）的概率框架，将测试时适应问题转化为状态估计与参数学习过程，涵盖了参数时间演化、先验调整及在线预测模型。

Result: 建立了参数学习与时变演化的统一概率建模方法，能够实现预测性能的持续优化。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的状态空间模型框架为在线测试时适应问题提供了统一且鲁棒的概率描述，能够有效处理分布偏移，并实现模型参数的自适应演化。

Abstract: This paper presents a probabilistic framework for online test-time adaptation problems. In them, a model is trained on labeled data but must adapt to unlabeled data at test time under the assumption that training and test distributions potentially differ, that is, there might have been a distributional shift. The framework is based on a state-space modelling architecture from which parameter learning, parameter time evolution, prior tuning, and prediction can be characterized.

</details>


### [181] [XMSE-Aware Adaptive Empirical Bayes Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26975)
*Minghao Chen,Jiale Zheng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对经验贝叶斯估计中“模型失配”问题的混合估计方法，通过XMSE感知权衡ML与EB的权重，保证了在核选择不当时估计器的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的经验贝叶斯（EB）估计器虽然在首阶渐近风险上与最大似然（ML）相当，但当核函数与真实参数不匹配时，其二阶风险（XMSE）可能显著差于ML。本文旨在解决这种由于核模型误设导致的性能退化问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于XMSE（过量均方误差）感知的混合估计器（mixed estimator），通过对ML和EB进行插值分配权重。利用有限样本XMSE近似实现了一种插件式（plug-in）算法，并证明了其在Oracle遗憾界的收敛性，同时扩展至紧凑核族及有限/增长核字典。

Result: 理论上证明了该估计器的二阶Oracle遗憾收敛性及其权重的选择界限。仿真实验和基准测试（Silverbox/Cascaded Tanks）显示，该模型在正则化有益时保持高精度，在模型不匹配时能自动向ML回归，具有更好的适应性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种XMSE感知混合估计器，它能有效平衡最大似然估计（ML）与经验贝叶斯（EB）收缩估计。该方法通过在核模型失配时退避至ML，在保持正则化优势的同时提高了估计的鲁棒性，并在多个基准测试中表现优于传统方法。

Abstract: Empirical Bayes (EB) estimators can match the first-order asymptotic risk of maximum likelihood (ML) while behaving very differently at second order: recent excess mean squared error (XMSE) analysis shows that kernel-based EB estimation may be worse than ML when the kernel is poorly aligned with the true parameter. This paper turns that diagnostic into a design principle. We propose an XMSE-aware mixed estimator that interpolates between ML and EB shrinkage. Its fixed-weight XMSE is a scalar quadratic, yielding a closed-form oracle mixing weight that is no worse than both ML and the base EB estimator at the XMSE scale. A plug-in implementation based on finite-sample XMSE approximations is proved consistent, with a second-order oracle regret rate for an interior oracle weight. We further establish a transfer of the regret bound to the fixed-weight risk curve evaluated at the selected weight, a thresholded boundary rule, and extensions to compact kernel families and to finite and growing kernel dictionaries with high-probability oracle bounds. Finite impulse response simulations with SURE-tuned, hard-selection, and trace-corrected baselines, together with the public Silverbox and Cascaded Tanks benchmarks, show that the proposed estimator retains most of the benefit of regularization when it is helpful and retreats toward ML under kernel misspecification, with an identified finite-de analyzed on the benchmarks.

</details>


### [182] [Beyond Global Divergences: A Local-Mass Perspective on Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27090)
*Hanli Xu,Fengxiang He,Sarat Moka*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文针对贝叶斯推断的全局目标函数无法描述“局部质量”的问题，提出了质量指数与正则化扩展KL散度（RE-KL）两套工具，用以量化并分析贝叶斯更新在局部尺度的分布行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的贝叶斯推断全局目标函数（如KL散度、ELBO）无法有效刻画分布的局部质量分布及变化行为，特别是在参数支持出现奇异性或局部缩放变化时。

Method: 1. 提出“质量指数 (Mass Index)”：用于量化局部质量的幂级和对数衰减尺度。
2. 提出“正则化扩展KL散度 (RE-KL)”：一种能够处理奇异分量并进行局部定位的散度度量。
3. 理论推导：证明了在使用两种KL方向度量局部小球质量时的绝对、相对及方向不等式。

Result: 提出了一套描述贝叶斯更新如何改变局部质量的理论机制（通过似然因子或参数化支持），证明了RE-KL在比较局部小球质量时的数学不等式，并通过实验验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文建立了一套分析贝叶斯推断中局部质量分布的理论框架，阐明了全局目标函数（如KL、ELBO）无法直接揭示的局部特性，并证明了局部RE-KL在度量局部小球质量方面的有效性。

Abstract: Global objectives, such as KL divergence and ELBO, are widely used in Bayesian inference for measuring distributional discrepancy. This paper studies their local-mass behaviour that is not directly captured by such objectives. We introduce and use two mathematical tools: (1) Mass Index for recording the polynomial and logarithmic decay scales of local mass, and (2) regularised extended KL (RE-KL), a set-localised divergence that can be formulated in the presence of singular components. Mass Indices help characterise how Bayesian updating changes local mass: (1) power-log likelihood factors shift it explicitly, and (2) parameter-dependent supports, or their smooth softenings, may change the local scale through the amount of mass that remains near the parameter value. Using local RE-KL, we prove absolute, relative, and directional inequalities for comparing local small-ball masses under the two KL directions. Together, these results provide a local theoretical account of local mass behaviour. Experiments provide controlled illustrations of the local behaviour. Code is available at https://github.com/Forsythia0604/Local-Mass-Framework.

</details>


### [183] [Ribbon: Scalable Approximation and Robust Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27269)
*Graham Gibson,John Tipton,Kellin Rumsey,Natalie Klein*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Ribbon 是一种用于深度学习模型的高效不确定性估计方法，通过影响函数线性化替代昂贵的多次模型重训练，实现了对 Dirichlet 加权自助法的快速近似，解决了高维模型下不确定性量化的算力瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的贝叶斯或自助法（Bootstrap）量化不确定性的方法在处理复杂、高维模型时计算开销过大，无法满足现代机器学习的需求。此外，模型在存在误设定（misspecified）时，传统的评估方法往往不够稳健，需要一种既能保持理论严谨性又能规模化部署的方法。

Method: 提出 Ribbon 方法：它利用影响函数（influence-function）线性化技术来近似 Dirichlet 加权自助法（Dirichlet-reweighted bootstrap）。通过在单个已训练模型的基础上进行后验线性代数运算，模拟多次模型重训练的效果，实现对不确定性的鲁棒估算。

Result: 在合成回归、MNIST 分类及加州房价预测等基准测试中，Ribbon 展现出与全模型重训练相当甚至更好的预测性能，有效改善了模型校准性，并极大地降低了计算时间成本。

Conclusion: Ribbon 提供了一种高效的、无需重新训练模型的预测不确定性量化方法。它在理论上与特定条件下的 Laplace 近似和稳健的 sandwich 协方差估计等价，在保持竞争力的预测性能的同时，显著提高了校准度。

Abstract: Reliably quantifying predictive uncertainty is difficult for complex, high-dimensional, or misspecified models. Both fully Bayesian and bootstrap resampling methods provide principled uncertainty estimates but are often too expensive for modern machine-learning models because they require posterior sampling or repeated model refitting. We introduce Ribbon, a scalable approximation to Dirichlet-reweighted bootstrap uncertainty. Ribbon replaces repeated refitting with an influence-function linearization around a single fitted model, preserving the first-order data-reweighting structure of the Bayesian bootstrap while requiring only post-hoc linear algebra. Ribbon approximates the Bayesian-bootstrap or weighted-likelihood-bootstrap refitting target. With a general concentration parameter, Ribbon gives a calibrated Dirichlet-reweighting family whose uncertainty scale can be tuned on validation data. We show that Ribbon is asymptotically equivalent to a flat-prior Laplace approximation under correct likelihood specification and recovers the robust sandwich covariance under misspecification. Across synthetic regression, MNIST classification, and California Housing benchmarks, Ribbon provides competitive predictive performance and improved calibration in several settings while avoiding repeated model retraining.

</details>


### [184] [When are likely answers right? On Sequence Probability and Correctness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27359)
*Johannes Zenn,Jonas Geiping*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 序列概率与生成内容的正确性并非简单的正相关关系：虽然其在宏观数据集上具有参考意义，但在具体的解码决策和单次采样评估中，其预测能力极弱甚至失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的许多大模型解码方法的核心逻辑是向高概率输出偏移，但“序列概率是否确实代表高质量或正确性”这一核心假设缺乏系统的实证研究。本文旨在探究序列概率与模型正确性之间的真实对齐程度。

Method: 研究团队通过对比不同解码方法、不同超参数设置、不同提示-回答对以及同一提示的多样化回答，在四个维度上量化了序列概率与模型生成内容正确性之间的相关性。

Result: 1. 同一数据集内，较高的序列概率确实能预测更高的正确性；2. 跨解码方法或通过调整超参数来提升序列概率，并不能稳定地提高准确性；3. 在针对同一提示的多次采样中，序列概率无法有效区分回答的正确与否。

Conclusion: 序列概率（sequence probability）与正确性之间的关系是高度情境化的。虽然在给定数据集内较高的序列概率往往预示着更高的准确率，但通过调整解码策略或超参数来强制提高概率并不能稳定地转化为准确率的提升。序列概率在同一问题的多次回答间也无法作为正确性的可靠指标。这提示研究者在进行解码策略优化及自一致性方法设计时需谨慎。

Abstract: Many decoding methods for large language models can be understood as shifting probability mass toward outputs that are more likely under the model, either locally at the token level or globally at the sequence level. Therefore, their success depends on a fundamental question: when does sequence probability, that is, the conditional probability of a continuation given a prompt, actually align with correctness? In this paper, we set out to quantify this relationship across decoding methods, models, and benchmarks at four levels: across decoding methods, across hyperparameters within a method, across prompt-answer pairs within a dataset, and across repeated responses to the same prompt. We find that higher sequence probability is often predictive of correctness across prompt-answer pairs within a fixed dataset. However, this relationship does not generally transfer to decoding decisions: increasing sequence probability by changing hyperparameters or methods does not reliably improve accuracy. Further, sequence probability is not a good indicator of correctness for responses to the same prompt. These findings clarify when decoding can and cannot be expected to improve correctness, and provide practical guidance for decoding, self-consistency, and verifier-free self-improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Query Cost Model Calibration in Confidential Virtual Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26385)
*Qihan Zhang,Mengyuan Li,Ibrahim Sabek*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对保密虚拟机（CVM）环境下数据库查询性能低下的问题，本文提出了一种轻量级的成本校准机制，通过修正优化器的成本假设，显著提升了CVM中的查询执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据库查询优化器在保密虚拟机（CVM）环境下基于非加密虚拟机的成本假设失效，导致查询性能严重下降，而现有研究多止步于基准测试，缺乏优化手段。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级的CVM感知成本校准模型。该模型针对数据移动和RMP转换导致的性能开销，利用查询优化器中已有的物理特征作为代理指标进行建模，从而更新查询优化器的成本假设。

Result: 实验证明该方法有效减少了KVM与CVM之间的性能差距，不仅显著提升了性能，在特定工作负载下还实现了对传统KVM环境的超越。

Conclusion: 通过引入轻量级的敏感虚拟机（CVM）感知成本校准机制，可以有效弥合数据库在CVM环境中与KVM环境的性能差距，最高可恢复48%的性能，甚至在部分工作负载下超越基准线。

Abstract: With the growing adoption of Confidential Computing, running databases in confidential virtual machines (CVMs) such as AMD SEV-SNP has become an attractive way to protect sensitive cloud data with minimal changes to legacy DBMSs. However, analytical queries in such CVMs often suffer substantial overhead, and prior database work has largely stopped at benchmarking these slowdowns rather than optimizing them. We show that this problem stems from a hardware-software mismatch: query optimizers still rely on KVM-oriented (non-encrypted VM) cost assumptions that no longer hold in CVMs. To address this, we propose a lightweight CVM-aware cost calibration. It models two dominant sources of optimizer-facing overhead: data movement and RMP-related translation using simple physical proxies already available to the optimizer. Experiments show that the calibration significantly narrows the KVM/CVM performance gap, recovering up to 48 percent performance and even outperforming the KVM baseline on some workloads.

</details>


### [186] [3D Spatial Pattern Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26465)
*Nicole R. Schneider,Avik Das,Lukas Arzoumanidis,Abhijeet Ghodgaonkar,Hanan Samet,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文将空间模式匹配扩展至三维空间，通过提出广义定义、设计三维子图匹配算法，并提供相关数据集，解决了二维模型在处理具有高度信息的实体时的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空间模式匹配方法局限于二维平面，无法有效处理具有高度属性的现实世界三维实体，限制了其在建筑及复杂城市环境搜索中的应用。

Method: 提出了一种针对三维空间的广义空间模式匹配定义，并开发了一种能够解析三维距离关系的空间子图匹配算法。

Result: 构建并发布了两个三维空间模式匹配数据集（一个合成数据集，一个基于德国汉堡真实三维建筑数据）；实验证明所提出的子图匹配算法能够有效处理三维空间模式。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将空间模式匹配从传统的二维扩展至三维，证实了该框架在处理具有高度信息的现实世界实体时的可行性与有效性，并为该领域的后续研究提供了基准数据集和算法基础。

Abstract: Spatial pattern matching is the process of matching query entities and constraints with database entities and relations. It has many applications, including similar region search, housing market search, landmark search, and road network matching. To our knowledge, all existing spatial pattern matching approaches frame the problem in a 2 dimensional space, where entities lie in a cartesian plane and relationships defined between them are contained in 2 dimensions. However, this problem framing has significant limitations when searching for real world entities that have height in addition to position. To address this limitation, we extend spatial pattern matching to 3 dimensions and provide a generalized definition of the problem. We describe a subgraph matching algorithm capable of resolving 3D spatial patterns over distance relations and release two 3D spatial pattern matching datasets, one synthetic and one containing real 3D building data from the city of Hamburg, Germany. We test our subgraph matching algorithm on both datasets and present results as a baseline for future methods to build upon.

</details>


### [187] [EcoTable: Cost-effective Table Integration in Data Lakes for Natural Language Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26613)
*Yuhui Wang,Jinqi Liu,Chengliang Chai,Hangyu Zhao,Yuhao Deng,Yuyu Luo,Xin Tang,Ye Yuan,Guoren Wang,Fengjin Wang,Lei Cao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: EcoTable 是首个基于自然语言的大规模数据集成框架，通过语义分析和图优化算法，自动化地将分散数据整合为满足分析需求的形态，显著提升了集成效率并大幅降低了 LLM 调用成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据湖中 CSV/Parquet 等多样化格式使得传统 ETL 手工定义 Schema 和 Pipeline 的方式难以应对多变的分析需求及不可预测的数据关联场景。

Method: 设计了基于图的数据搜索空间（节点为表，边权为连接倾向性）。核心组件包括：1) 基于两阶段语义映射的表识别层；2) 基于施坦纳树（Steiner tree）搜索的图验证层（处理连接路径与转换逻辑）；3) 基于 LLM 的表转换代码生成层。

Result: 在 4 个真实场景数据集及 200+ 查询基准上测试，EcoTable 与 SOTA 基线相比准确性提升超过 30%，LLM 推理成本降低至原来的 1/5。

Conclusion: EcoTable 基于自然语言查询实现了数据集成自动化。通过引入图结构及针对性的组件设计，在提升集成准确性的同时，有效降低了 LLM 的计算与财务成本，证明了在大规模异构数据上实现自动化数据集成的可行性。

Abstract: The diverse formats of CSV and Parquet files in data lakes pose a significant challenge to traditional ETL, which relies on data engineers to pre-define a target database schema and build a complex pipeline for data integration. Moreover, with this approach, the integrated data often cannot support various analytical needs, as the predefined schema does not necessarily satisfy the table format or join relationships required to answer unforeseen queries. To address this, we propose EcoTable, the first natural language-based data integration framework. Given a set of user-specified natural language queries, EcoTable automatically integrates the tables into a form that adequately supports the corresponding SQL queries. EcoTable achieves this by leveraging the semantic understanding and complex reasoning capabilities of LLMs. Moreover, EcoTable addresses the scalability and cost issues introduced by expensive LLM inferences with a set of novel ideas. First, EcoTable introduces a graph to represent the overall search space, where nodes represent tables and edges carry weights indicating join likelihood produced by a lightweight deep learning model. On top of this graph data structure, EcoTable designs three components to achieve our goal: (1) the table identification layer aims to identify relevant tables via a two-stage schema linking based on user queries; (2) the graph-based validation layer aims to discover significant join paths, including necessary data transformations and bridging tables, by modeling the problem as Steiner tree searches; and (3) the table transformation layer generates transformation code to implement the joins using LLMs. We construct 4 real-world benchmark datasets with more than 200 queries. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EcoTable outperforms the state-of-the-art baselines, increasing accuracy by more than 30% and cutting LLM invocation costs by 5 times.

</details>


### [188] [BtrLog: Low-Latency Logging for Cloud Database Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27051)
*Maximilian Kuschewski,Lam-Duy Nguyen,Matthias Jasny,Tobias Ziegler,Viktor Leis,Muhammad El-Hindi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 为解决云原生数据库日志存储（WAL）在高延迟、高成本与系统耦合度之间的矛盾，BtrLog 提供了一种基于 SSD 和对象存储分层架构的高性能、可复用日志服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生数据库使用传统的块存储（如 EBS）存在高延迟和高成本问题，而对象存储又不适用于 OLTP（高延迟、单次追加成本高）；此外，现有的专用日志后端与特定存储引擎耦合紧密，难以复用。

Method: 1. 采用分布式架构，将日志在 SSD 支撑的节点集群中进行副本存储，确保低延迟； 2. 引入单网络往返（single network round trip）的共识机制以减少对慢节点（stragglers）的依赖； 3. 采用分层存储策略，异步将日志记录以大块（segment）形式归档到对象存储，从而降低存储成本。

Result: 在评估中，BtrLog 的延迟表现优于 EBS，并显著提升了数据库管理系统的端到端事务吞吐量。

Conclusion: BtrLog 通过创新的架构设计，有效解决了云原生数据库在 WAL 持久化中面临的延迟、成本与解耦困境，是一个高性能、低成本且可复用的云原生日志记录服务解决方案。

Abstract: Cloud database systems cannot rely on instance-local disks for write-ahead logging (WAL) durability, forcing WAL onto remote storage. Existing options are unsatisfying: remote block storage like EBS is easy to adopt but adds substantial write latency and cost, while object storage offers excellent durability and low storage cost but is impractical for OLTP due to high latency and per-append cost. Many cloud-native databases, therefore, depend on purpose-built logging backends, which are typically proprietary and tightly coupled to engine-specific replication and recovery protocols, limiting reuse. We present BtrLog, a reusable cloud logging service that combines low-latency durable appends with low-cost archival for the common single-writer architecture. BtrLog replicates log records across a quorum of SSD-backed log nodes in a single network round trip, reducing sensitivity to stragglers in commit latency. To minimize storage cost, log nodes archive records to object storage as large segments, which are written asynchronously and off the latency-critical write path. In our evaluation, BtrLog achieves lower latency than EBS and enables higher end-to-end transaction throughput when integrated into a DBMS.

</details>


### [189] [Understanding Domain-Aware Distribution Alignment in Budgeted Entity Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27342)
*Nicholas Pulsone,Gregory Goren,Roee Shraga*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文深入研究了低资源领域感知实体匹配框架BEACON，评估了其在不同算法设置与数据 availability 条件下的表现，阐明了分布对齐等机制对性能的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然现有研究通过引入领域信息和低资源学习提升了实体匹配（EM）性能，但这些方法在实际应用中面对不同数据制约和监督程度时的稳健性与行为机制尚不明确。

Method: 对现有的先进低资源、领域感知实体匹配模型（BEACON）进行变体实验与对比研究，重点考察分布对齐（distribution alignment）机制及不同算法配置（algorithmic choices）在不同数据限制条件下的表现。

Result: 明确了BEACON框架在不同数据约束下的行为特征，特别是量化了分布对齐技术对模型性能的具体影响，为优化低资源EM系统提供了指导。

Conclusion: 本文通过对BEACON模型的深入分析，揭示了算法选择与数据可用性如何共同影响领域感知实体匹配的性能，为低资源场景下的实体匹配提供了关键的实证洞察。

Abstract: Entity Matching (EM) is a core operation in the data integration pipeline, where records from different sources are compared to determine whether they refer to the same real-world entity. Recent work has incorporated domain information and low-resource learning techniques to better adapt EM systems to realistic settings. While these approaches have demonstrated strong performance, it remains unclear how they behave under varying data constraints and levels of supervision in practice. In this paper, we investigate a state-of-the-art method for low-resource, domain-aware EM--BEACON--and study how its performance is affected by different algorithmic choices and data availability conditions. We conduct a series of targeted experiments to evaluate these variations, providing deeper insight into the role of distribution alignment and the behavior of the BEACON framework.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Hot AI in Cold Space: Thermal-Crosstalk-Aware Scheduling for Sustainable Orbital AI Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26150)
*Shuyi Chen,Zhengchang Hua,Nikos Tziritas,Georgios Theodoropoulos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文旨在解决轨道数据中心因高密度部署引发的热管理危机。通过提出的TLB软件框架，根据热状态动态分配工作负载，在确保AI训练效率的同时，通过缓解热疲劳减少太空电子垃圾，实现轨道AI的可持续化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面人工智能训练面临能源与水资源危机，虽然轨道数据中心（ODCs）是零碳替代方案，但分布式AI训练要求极低延迟，导致的“邻近-热悖论”引发热交叉耦合及热疲劳，造成硬件寿命骤减和太空电子垃圾。

Method: 提出“热感知异构性（Thermal-Aware Heterogeneity Thesis）”，即视空间冷却差异为资源管理维度，并设计了“热负载平衡（Thermal-Load Balancing, TLB）”软件框架，根据实时流体温度或辐射吸收量动态迁移大型语言模型（LLM）训练工作负载。

Result: 分析表明，TLB框架能有效解决热瓶颈，恢复模型算力利用率（MFU），降低硬件热应力，并延长轨道硬件的使用寿命，从而摊薄火箭发射的隐含碳成本。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了“热感知异构性”论点，并引入了“热负载平衡（TLB）”框架，通过动态工作负载迁移解决轨道数据中心的散热瓶颈，从而在维持高训练吞吐量的同时延长硬件寿命，是实现可持续轨道人工智能的关键路径。

Abstract: Terrestrial AI training faces an unsustainable energy and water crisis, positioning Orbital Data Centers (ODCs) as a "zero operational carbon" alternative. However, the sub-$10μ\text{s}$ communication latency required for distributed Large Language Model (LLM) training forces ODCs into extreme physical density, triggering a critical "Proximity-Thermal Paradox." As these high-density systems scale into Monolithic Structures or Proximity Swarms, they suffer from intense thermal-fluid crosstalk (heat traps in shared cooling loops) and thermal-radiative crosstalk (mutual heating that blocks deep-space cooling radiators). If left unmitigated, this persistent heat stagnation not only triggers severe thermal throttling that degrades training throughput, but also induces severe thermal fatigue, drastically shortening hardware lifespans and generating premature space e-waste. To make orbital AI truly sustainable, this position paper challenges traditional uniform load-sharing. We propose the Thermal-Aware Heterogeneity Thesis, which treats spatial cooling variances as a primary resource management dimension. Building on this, we introduce Thermal-Load Balancing (TLB), a software framework that dynamically migrates LLM workloads to the coolest available units based on instantaneous fluid temperatures or absorbed radiation. Our analysis demonstrates that TLB resolves thermal bottlenecks to restore Model Flops Utilization (MFU), while simultaneously reducing physical thermal stress. Extending the operational lifespan of orbital hardware is crucial to amortize the massive embodied carbon of rocket launches, outlining a necessary pathway to scale orbital AI without accelerating e-waste.

</details>


### [191] [FinWhale: An Optimally Resilient Two-Round Terminating DAG Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26292)
*Razya Ladelsky,Roy Friedman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FinWhale 是首个将快速路径（两消息延迟）集成到 DAG 结构中的拜占庭容错协议，通过扩展 Mysticeti 并引入新的提交结构，在保证安全性的前提下实现了低延迟与高吞吐量的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 DAG 的拜占庭容错 (BFT) 协议在大吞吐量方面表现优异，但在部分同步模型下提交决策通常需要至少三个消息延迟；而能够实现最优两消息延迟的“快速路径 (fast path)”BFT 协议难以直接扩展到 DAG 结构中。FinWhale 旨在解决这一矛盾，实现兼具高吞吐量与最优延迟的 DAG 共识。

Method: FinWhale 基于 Mysticeti 协议进行扩展，引入了一种新的共识机制：利用基于“快速路径证据块 (fast path evidence blocks)”的提交结构，使得验证者能够安全地结合快速路径与原始慢速路径的逻辑，并确保不同局部 DAG 视图下的一致性。该协议在 n = 3f + 2p - 1 个验证者规模下运行。

Result: FinWhale 是首个支持两消息延迟快速路径的 DAG-BFT 协议。它在容忍最多 f 个拜占庭故障的同时，若快速路径期间发生故障的验证者数量 p (1 <= p <= f) 满足条件，即可实现快速终止。该协议达到了快速拜占庭共识的已知下界。

Conclusion: FinWhale 证明了最优延迟的快速路径可以集成到未认证的 DAG 共识协议中，实现了在部分同步模型下，DAG 结构与两消息延迟终止的有机结合。

Abstract: DAG based Byzantine Fault Tolerant protocols provide high throughput consensus under partial synchrony but existing DAG protocols still require at least three message delays to commit decisions. In contrast fast path Byzantine Fault Tolerant protocols can achieve optimal two message delay termination under favorable conditions though they do not naturally extend to DAGs.
  We present FinWhale the first DAG based Byzantine Fault Tolerant protocol with a two message delay fast path. FinWhale extends Mysticeti with a novel fast path commit mechanism that safely coexists with the protocol's original slow path rules. To preserve safety across different local DAG views we introduce new commit structures based on fast path evidence blocks enabling validators to combine fast path and slow path reasoning consistently.
  FinWhale operates in the partially synchronous model with n equals three f plus two p minus one validators matching the known lower bound for fast Byzantine consensus. The protocol tolerates up to f Byzantine faults and achieves fast termination whenever at most p validators fail during the fast path where p is between one and f. Our results show that optimal latency fast paths can be integrated into uncertified DAG consensus protocols.

</details>


### [192] [A Distributed Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm Simulator for Engineering Design Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26297)
*Ali Rajabi,Milad Hasanzadeh,Amin Kargarian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文开发了一款开源分布式QAOA模拟器，支持单片与多QPU分布式执行，通过电路复用和并行优化提升了大规模QUBO问题的求解效率，并已成功应用于实际工业场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏可复用的仿真工具来解决并在不同模式（单片式与分布式）下对比QAOA执行效果，以处理工程设计和决策中产生的QUBO问题。

Method: 构建了一个兼容Qiskit的分布式QAOA模拟框架。核心处理流程包括：QUBO模型的规范化、映射至成本哈密顿量、变量在多QPU间的划分、本地与跨QPU耦合的识别及电路构造；并引入了参数化电路复用、批量评估及并行多启动执行等运行时优化策略。

Result: 该模拟器在QUBO基准测试及电力机组组合应用中与经典单片式QAOA结果一致。运行时分析显示，尽管跨QPU耦合存在远程通信开销，但通过算法优化显著降低了整体运行时间。提供了Streamlit图形界面，提高了用户易用性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的分布式QAOA模拟器成功实现了QUBO问题的分布式求解，验证了其在解决实际工程问题（如电力机组组合）时的准确性，并证明了通过多种优化策略可显著提升运行效率，为大规模量子计算模拟提供了有效工具。

Abstract: This paper presents a Qiskit-compatible distributed quantum approximate optimization algorithm (DQAOA) simulator for quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problems arising in engineering design and decision applications. The open-source simulator is available through the RAISE LAB website and GitHub repository, with README documentation for installation, input formatting, configurable parameters, and example workflows. The package addresses the need for a reusable simulator that can solve and compare QUBO instances across different QAOA execution modes. It supports monolithic QAOA on a single quantum processing unit (QPU) and distributed QAOA across a user-specified number of QPUs with configurable capacities. The workflow canonicalizes the QUBO model, maps it to a cost Hamiltonian, allocates variables across QPUs, identifies local and cross-QPU couplings, and constructs the corresponding circuits. Runtime optimizations, including parameterized circuit reuse, objective reuse at fixed depth, batched evaluations, and parallel multi-start execution, reduce repeated overhead. A Streamlit graphical user interface is also provided for entering or uploading QUBO instances, configuring solver settings, running selected modes, and visualizing solution-quality metrics without editing Python scripts. The package is demonstrated on standalone QUBO benchmarks and a power generation unit commitment application. In the unit commitment case, brute force, monolithic QAOA, and distributed QAOA recover the same commitment bitstring and operating cost. Across multiple case studies, the simulator produces results consistent with classical monolithic QAOA references in terms of optimal bitstrings and costs. Staged runtime analysis shows substantial runtime reduction across implementation stages, while distributed QAOA remains more demanding because cross-QPU couplings require remote operations.

</details>


### [193] [Priceless: An examination of Serverless Functions-as-a-Service (FaaS) pricing models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26308)
*Nnamdi Ekwe-Ekwe*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Priceless框架，通过对AWS、Azure和Google Cloud的FaaS定价模型进行横向对比分析，发现尽管配置相似，但不同厂商与区域间的成本差异巨大，其中AWS成本最低，Azure最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年FaaS平台功能激增，导致了复杂且差异巨大的定价模型，用户难以直观评估和选择成本最优的方案。

Method: 通过调研当前主流FaaS平台的功能与定价模型，构建对比分析框架，并针对典型工作负载在AWS、Azure和Google Cloud三大平台及不同区域进行实际运行成本对比。

Result: 研究揭示了跨云厂商和跨区域部署在成本上的显著差异，量化了三大主流云服务商在相同工作负载下的价格表现。

Conclusion: FaaS平台的定价模型存在显著复杂性，跨云厂商及跨区域间存在巨大的成本差异。总体而言，AWS Lambda最经济，而Azure Functions成本最高。

Abstract: Serverless Functions-as-a-Service providers have grown in their offering since inception a decade ago, with a myriad of new functionalities offered to end-users. These new features have also brought new, varied and at times complex pricing models that differ between providers. In this paper, we present Priceless, a detailed examination of the current state of the art of FaaS features, and their pricing models. We then perform a comparative price analysis of running example workloads across AWS Lambda, Microsoft Azure Functions and Google Cloud Functions. Our work finds significant cost differences both cross-provider, but also, cross-region within the provider. We find that AWS is the cheapest overall to run functions on, with Microsoft Azure being the most expensive for equivalent workloads.

</details>


### [194] [Moebius: Serving Mixture-of-Expert Models with Seamless Runtime Parallelism Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26607)
*Shaoyu Wang,Yizhuo Liang,Jaeyong Song,Chong Li,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Moebius 是一个支持 MoE 模型运行时动态切换 EP 和 TP 并行策略的推理系统，解决了静态划分在波动工作负载下的性能缺陷，实现了高效的在线重分片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 MoE 模型推理系统在 Tensor Parallelism (TP) 和 Expert Parallelism (EP) 之间做静态选择，无法应对生产环境中波动的并发请求量，导致性能损失。

Method: 提出 Moebius 系统，利用 EP 和 TP 共享模型权重和 KV 缓存的特性，在运行时通过融合的 GPU 到 GPU 传输内核进行模型权重的重分片（Resharding），实现并行策略的动态切换，无需重启系统或中断请求。

Result: 在 Qwen3-235B-A22B 模型上，Moebius 在所有工作负载下均能达到或超过最佳静态并行策略的性能，特别是在强化学习（RL）场景下性能提升了 1.16-1.25 倍，且单次切换延迟仅为 215-434 毫秒。

Conclusion: Moebius 能够实现 EP 和 TP 之间的无缝实时切换，在不损失性能的前提下适应工作负载的波动，通过仅 2.4% 的内存开销换取了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) to hundreds of billions of parameters. Serving a single MoE model requires multiple GPUs operating in parallel, typically through tensor parallelism (TP) or expert parallelism (EP). The optimal choice depends on the number of in-flight requests: TP is faster at low concurrency, whereas EP wins at high concurrency. Production workloads cross this boundary continually: online serving sees bursty arrivals that subside into quiet periods, and reinforcement-learning rollouts begin as a high-concurrency burst that decays into a long tail of stragglers. Pinning either layout therefore forfeits performance when the workload crosses to the other side.
  We present Moebius, a serving system that switches between EP and TP at runtime without restarting the engine or dropping in-flight requests. Our key insight is that EP and TP are two layouts of one model, not two models: they compute the same function over byte-identical expert weights and KV cache, so a switch changes only which rank owns each slice. Moving those owner-changed slices is the sole irreducible cost, and modern high-bandwidth GPU interconnects make it fast enough to do between decode steps without draining in-flight requests. Moebius preserves each parallelism's runtime resident, and reshards the single copy of expert weights and KV cache at fixed addresses with fused GPU-to-GPU transfer kernels. On 8x H200 GPUs serving Qwen3-235B-A22B, Moebius matches the better static parallelism at every operating point, and beats it on RL rollouts by 1.16-1.25x across steps. Each switch completes in 215-434 ms, and Moebius holds both layouts resident with only 2.4% memory overhead.

</details>


### [195] [Simulating Unified Tensor Resharding in heterogeneous AI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26633)
*Sumit Kumar,Sayantan Dasgupta,Kushal Mitra,Meet Dadhania,Rohan Sudhir Basugade,Praveen Tammana,Satananda Burla,Abed Mohammad Kamaluddin,Rinku Shah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Xsim 是一款针对分布式大模型训练的异构感知仿真器，通过支持异构硬件与网络建模，实现了对复杂现实生产环境的高精度性能预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前仿真器多假设计算与网络基础设施是均匀的，但现实中模型架构（如 MoE）、公有云硬件可用性及地理分布式部署使得异构性成为常态，现有工具无法准确建模这种异构性。

Method: 1. 支持非均匀工作负载划分以实现负载均衡；2. 构建异构感知的集合通信模型（自定义环构造和分块）；3. 提供可重用的异构感知抽象（用于流水线并行和张量重切分）；4. 灵活的输入抽象（支持自定义设备组与并行映射）；5. 可插拔架构（与 NS-3 和 htsim 集成以权衡精度与性能）。

Result: Xsim 在多种异构数据并行/张量并行配置下的预测误差小于 5%，在流水线并行通信建模中的误差约为 2%，并能有效暴露流水线气泡时间和滞后节点等待时间等关键指标。

Conclusion: Xsim 通过支持异构计算和网络环境下的建模，为分布式大模型训练提供了一种高保真、可扩展的仿真手段，证明了考虑异构特性的仿真对于准确预测训练时间和优化资源配置至关重要。

Abstract: State-of-the-art AI training simulators assume homogeneous compute and network infrastructure. However, real-world training infrastructure is becoming increasingly heterogeneous since: (a) Model architectures such as multimodal and MoE exploit heterogeneity to improve device utilization, (b) Public cloud platforms often provide limited availability of homogeneous hardware due to fast hardware evolution, and (c) Large enterprises frequently deploy geographically distributed infrastructure that is both diverse and heterogeneous. In this paper, we present Xsim, a heterogeneity-aware simulator for distributed LLM training. Xsim supports: (i) Load balancing through non-uniform workload partitioning across heterogeneous device groups, (ii) Heterogeneity-aware collective communication via customized ring construction and chunk partitioning, (iii) Reusable heterogeneity-aware abstractions for emerging pipeline-parallel algorithms and non-uniform tensor resharding technique, (iv) Flexible input abstractions for specifying deployment plans with custom device groups and custom device-to-parallelism mappings, and (v) Pluggable integration with NS-3 and htsim, allowing users to trade off simulation fidelity for performance and scalability. Our evaluation demonstrates that Xsim accurately predicts training time for real-world heterogeneous deployments, with an error of less than 5% across most heterogeneous data-parallel/tensor-parallel configurations and around 2% error with pipeline-parallel communication modeling. We expose actionable metrics such as pipeline bubble time and straggler waiting time.

</details>


### [196] [RolloutPipe: Overlapping Pipelined Rollout and Training in Disaggregated On-Policy LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26997)
*Rongjian Chen,Jianmin Hu,Kejiang Ye,Minxian Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RolloutPipe 是一款用于离散化 RLVR 系统的流水线框架，通过 CGP 和 FGD 技术实现 rollout 与训练的重叠，在保持同策略正确性的同时大幅提高了训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离散化架构的 RLVR 系统中，现有的同步 on-policy 方法（如 GRPO）存在 rollout 与训练阶段阻塞导致的 trainer 计算资源闲置问题，而异步方法虽然解决了闲置，却引入了训练“过时数据”（stale data）的副作用。

Method: 提出 RolloutPipe 框架，包含两项核心技术：1. Complete-group pipelining (CGP)：允许生成完成的组立即进入 trainer 进行训练，而非等待所有组完成；2. Frontier-group dispatch (FGD)：一种调度策略，优先处理形成下一批训练数据所需的“前沿组”，确保训练数据的持续供给。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B 模型及多个推理与科学基准测试上，RolloutPipe 将从 rollout 到训练结束的时间缩短了 30.7%-42.3%，并将 trainer 的空闲比率降低了 37%-76%。

Conclusion: RolloutPipe 通过引入 complete-group pipelining (CGP) 和 frontier-group dispatch (FGD) 技术，在保持同策略（on-policy）准确性的前提下，打破了传统 RLVR 系统中“滚动生成-训练”的阻塞式流程，有效提升了训练资源利用率并缩短了训练周期。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) post-training for reasoning increasingly relies on reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), where models learn from ground-truth feedback on mathematical, logical, and scientific tasks. To enable flexible resource allocation and support heterogeneous training setups, modern RLVR systems adopt disaggregated architectures that decouple rollout generation and policy training across independent GPU pools. However, existing synchronous on-policy GRPO (Group Relative Policy Optimization) RLVR systems finish an entire rollout before starting training, leaving the trainer GPU pool idle while rollout is still ongoing. Asynchronous RL pipelines overlap the two stages, but at the cost of training on stale data. To address these challenges, we propose RolloutPipe, a post-training framework for disaggregated RLVR systems, which turns the fixed-weight rollout into a complete-group pipeline where trainable groups move to the trainer while later groups are still being generated. RolloutPipe achieves this through two techniques including complete-group pipelining (CGP) and frontier-group dispatch (FGD). CGP dispatches each trainable complete group to the trainer FIFO as soon as group materialization finishes, and FGD is an admission policy on the Rollout node that first admits requests for the frontier groups needed to form the next training batch, so that trainer-ready groups arrive earlier and more steadily. The design starts training before the rollout completes while maintaining on-policy correctness. Evaluated on Qwen3-1.7B across four reasoning and science benchmarks and twelve rollout settings, RolloutPipe shortens the rollout-to-train-end time by 30.7%-42.3%, and lowers the trainer waiting ratio by 37%-76% compared to Slime, a state-of-the-art rollout and training system.

</details>


### [197] [DMuon: Efficient Distributed Muon Training with Near-Adam Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27153)
*Vincent Chen,Starrick Liu,Regis Cheng,Dance Yang,Shalfun Li,Ryan Yu,Lucy Liang,Hang Su,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DMuon 是一款高效的分布式 Muon 优化器实现，通过大幅降低计算开销，解决了 Muon 在主流分布式训练架构中性能不佳的问题，实现了与 AdamW 相媲美的每步训练延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于矩阵正交化的优化器（如 Muon）在大规模模型训练中具备优异的收敛性，但由于其不仅涉及矩阵级耦合计算，且原有的 Newton-Schulz 迭代开销极高（超过前向/反向传播 2 倍以上），现有的分布式训练基础设施（多针对元素级优化器设计）难以高效运行此类算法。

Method: 提出 DMuon，一种高性能的分布式 Muon 实现。该方案通过优化 Newton-Schulz 迭代过程，使其能作为即插即用的模块集成到现有训练流水线中，无需框架层面的修改。

Result: 在具身智能基础模型和 LLM 训练任务中，DMuon 实现了 1.48x-3.01x 的端到端步时加速，以及 6.85x-163.00x 的优化器步时加速，性能表现优异。

Conclusion: DMuon 成功解决了现有分布式训练基础设施对矩阵级别优化器支持不足的问题，通过高性能的实现使其在各类大规模模型训练中具备了极高的实用价值和扩展性，实现了接近 AdamW 的每步延迟。

Abstract: Matrix-orthogonalization-based optimizers, exemplified by Muon, have demonstrated strong convergence behavior across a wide range of modern deep learning workloads. The matrix-aware updates offer a compelling alternative to conventional element-wise optimization, particularly as model architectures continue to grow in scale and heterogeneity. Yet contemporary distributed training infrastructure built around the assumption of element-wise optimizers is poorly matched to matrix-level optimizers such as Muon, whose updates couple entire weight matrices and require costly Newton-Schulz iterations. Vanilla Muon implementations incur more than 2x the cost of forward and backward passes. To close this gap, we present DMuon, an open-source distributed Muon implementation that integrates into existing training pipelines as a drop-in module, with no framework-level modifications. Across both embodied foundation model and large language model (LLM) training workloads, DMuon achieves a 1.48x-3.01x speedup in end-to-end step time and a 6.85x-163.00x speedup in optimizer-step time, bringing per-step latency to near-AdamW levels and enabling efficient scaling in our model training.

</details>


### [198] [CHAMB-GA: A Containerized HPC Scalable Microservice-Based Framework for Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27217)
*Felix Bonhoff,Thiemo Pesch,Andrea Benigni,Alexander Mitsos,Manuel Dahmen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CHAMB-GA 是一种基于微服务和容器化的优化框架，将遗传算法与仿真任务解耦，支持从个人电脑到大规模 HPC 集群的无缝迁移与弹性扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决元启发式算法在集成复杂仿真时面临的计算开销大、工具链硬件绑定、可扩展性差以及难以从个人电脑向分布式集群无缝迁移的问题。

Method: 提出 CHAMB-GA 框架，采用基于消息代理（Message Broker）的异步微服务架构，将遗传算法算子与仿真后端解耦；利用容器编排（如 Kubernetes）在云端部署，并支持通过批处理调度（如 SLURM）在 HPC 集群上执行。

Result: 成功验证了该框架在基准测试中可扩展至超过 3,500 个 CPU 核心且开销最小；在德国高压直流（HVDC）电网调度应用中，实现了从云端到 HPC 集群的无缝迁移及多阶段工作流集成。

Conclusion: CHAMB-GA 证明了在不依赖特定硬件的前提下，通过容器化微服务架构可以实现元启发式算法与复杂仿真任务的跨平台高效扩展，为工业级电力系统优化提供了灵活且可复现的解决方案。

Abstract: Metaheuristic-based global optimization with embedded, long-running simulations is a computationally expensive process. To support various stages of development and execution, a seamless transition from personal computers to distributed clusters is desired, enabling execution across all computational scales. However, existing tool chains are often characterized by rigidity and hardware-bound constraints, which impede scalability and the integration of complex simulations. Bridging this gap, we present a containerized HPC scalable microservice-based framework for genetic algorithms with embedded simulations (CHAMB-GA). The deployment of the framework scales consistently across cloud infrastructure via container orchestration and HPC clusters via batch-scheduled parallel execution. Users provide the GA operators and simulation backend separately. The framework is designed to run these components in a distributed and decoupled manner, mapped to separate hardware. This approach ensures that the fitness evaluation and genetic operations are not managed within the same process and are utilizing distinct parts of the compute infrastructure. A central message broker coordinates asynchronous manager-worker communication between microservices, thereby parallelizing evolutionary operations and fitness evaluations. We demonstrate CHAMB-GA's scalability, portability, and reproducibility, while facilitating the integration of external tools and complex simulations on benchmark and powerflow problems. The capabilities of CHAMB-GA are validated in a two-part approach: (i) a benchmark study demonstrating minimal overhead while scaling to over 3,500 CPU cores, and (ii) a dispatch optimization of High Voltage Direct Current (HVDC) lines in the German transmission grid, showing seamless migration from Kubernetes to SLURM, combined horizontal and vertical scaling, and integration of multi-stage workflows.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Reducing Redundancy in Whole-Slide Image Patching for Scalable Indexing and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26157)
*Jialiang Geng,Ghazal Alabtah,Saghir Alfasly,Wataru Uegami,H. R. Tizhoosh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 为解决全切片图像（WSI）高昂的存储成本问题，本文提出 ARReST 框架，通过剔除对跨类区分贡献极小的“对立补丁”，在显著压缩存储空间的同时保持了高效的检索准确度，为临床 AI 提供了经济高效的索引方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字病理学的发展对全切片图像（WSI）的高效索引和检索提出了迫切需求，尤其是在检索增强生成（RAG）等 AI 工作流中。然而，高性能存储的高昂成本限制了 WSI 索引的扩展性，因此需要一种既能降低存储需求又能保证检索准确性的方法。

Method: 提出了一种名为 ARReST（Antithetical Redundancy Reduction Strategy）的对抗性冗余减少策略。该框架利用不同组织类别之间的冗余，识别并剪除对跨类别区分贡献极小的“对立补丁（antithetical patches）”，从而在减少索引数据量的同时，保持了形态多样性和检索保真度。

Result: 在 TCGA 数据集（涉及 21 个器官）上的实验表明，ARReST 在不影响检索性能的情况下，实现了 3% 至 60%（平均 14%±13%）的存储节省，显著降低了计算开销并加速了相似度搜索。

Conclusion: ARReST 为全切片图像（WSI）的索引提供了一种可扩展且具有成本效益的解决方案，非常适合下一代检索驱动的临床 AI 系统。

Abstract: The rapid growth of digital pathology has created an urgent need for efficient indexing and retrieval of whole slide images (WSIs). This need is intensified by emerging generative AI workflows, particularly retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), which require dependable similarity search to support high-stakes clinical decision-making. Yet the substantial cost of high-performance storage limits the scalability and accessibility of WSI indexing for many healthcare institutions. Consequently, methods that can reduce storage demands while preserving retrieval accuracy have become a critical research priority. We propose ARReST (Antithetical Redundancy Reduction Strategy), a principled oppositional framework that leverages redundancy across dissimilar tissue classes to markedly decrease the number of patches that must be indexed from each WSI. Instead of eliminating only within-class duplicates, ARReST identifies antithetical patches-those whose representations contribute minimally to cross-class discrimination-and prunes them from the searchable archive. This targeted reduction substantially compresses the index without sacrificing morphological diversity or retrieval fidelity. By minimizing superfluous patch representations, ARReST reduces storage footprint, lowers computational overhead, and accelerates similarity search across large pathology repositories. Extensive experiments on TCGA repository (The Cancer Genome Atlas with 21 organs) demonstrate that ARReST achieves significant index compression while maintaining competitive retrieval performance. The observed storage savings of 3% to 60% (14%$\pm$13%) can be reliably achieved without compromising retrieval performance for many organs. The proposed strategy enables scalable, cost-efficient WSI indexing and is well-suited for next-generation retrieval-driven clinical AI systems.

</details>


### [200] [From Clicks to Intent: Cross-Platform Session Embeddings with LLM-Distilled Taxonomy for Financial Services Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26277)
*Dianjing Fan,Yao Li,Kyaw Hpone Myint,Dwipam Katariya,Alexandre G. R. Day,Pranab Mohanty,Giri Iyengar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对金融行业跨渠道用户行为割裂问题，本文提出了一种结合自监督Transformer嵌入与LLM蒸馏标签的框架，在提升推荐精准度的同时兼顾了意图的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融领域中，匿名网页浏览（探索）与已登录App操作（服务）行为存在差异，由于跨渠道实体对齐困难，导致网页端意图信号在个性化推荐中利用不足，且现有方法难以兼顾定量推荐与定性分析。

Method: 提出了一种双重意图预测框架：包含基于自监督Transformer的点击流编码器（生成会话Embedding）和基于LLM的分类与蒸馏流水线（生成可解释意图标签）。

Result: 在真实生产环境中，会话Embedding在首页推荐任务中提升了1.88%的Recall@1并降低了13.38%的Log Loss；用户转化预测中，Embedding性能优于LLM标签，而经过蒸馏的LLM标签实现了极低延迟下的可解释输出。

Conclusion: 本文提出的双重意图预测框架有效利用了金融场景下被忽视的匿名网页浏览行为，实现了推荐系统定量精准度与定性可解释性的双重提升。

Abstract: Sequential user behavior modeling is widely adopted in industrial recommender systems; however, significant gaps remain in financial services, where pre-login web interactions and authenticated in-app experiences differ drastically. Specifically, pre-login web users typically explore new products, whereas logged-in app users focus on account servicing. Due to the challenge of cross-channel entity resolution (e.g., matching anonymous web sessions to authenticated mobile accounts), web-based intent signals remain underutilized for post-authentication personalization. Existing methods for capturing web-based intent are often ad-hoc and narrow, lacking the flexibility to support both quantitative downstream recommendations and qualitative understanding at scale. In this work, we propose a scalable and dual-purpose intent prediction framework for web-based interactions and demonstrate its applicability for personalization. Our approach transforms raw web clickstreams into two outputs: a self-supervised Transformer encodes multi-modal clickstreams into a compact session embedding, while an LLM-based taxonomy generation and distillation pipeline produces interpretable intent labels. Our system demonstrates that self-supervised clickstream representations combined with LLM-distilled taxonomies can jointly serve quantitative tasks and qualitative understanding in production: on the mobile homepage tile ranking task, the session embedding improves macro Recall@1 by 1.88% and reduces Log Loss by 13.38% over production baselines. On the user conversion prediction task, the embedding outperforms the LLM labels by 4.3% on micro F1, while the distillation layer delivers interpretable labels at ultra-low latency with only a 7% performance drop.

</details>


### [201] [Scoring Is Not Enough: Addressing Gaps in Utility-fairness Trade-offs for Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26369)
*Shubham Singh,Ian A. Kash,Mesrob I. Ohannessian*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“公平性与效用权衡应通过学习评分函数实现”这一传统观点，证明了基于评分的排名存在本质缺陷，并提出通过半贪婪后处理方法来获得更好的公平-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的信息检索和推荐系统常通过学习评分函数来优化效用，但当引入算法公平性约束时，基于评分的排名方法是否仍是实现效用-公平性最优权衡的最佳方案尚不明确。

Method: 通过一系列反例进行理论证明，分析确定性和随机性评分函数在单查询及多查询场景下的公平性表现；并提出并实证评估了“半贪婪后处理”（semi-greedy post-processing）方法。

Result: 理论上证明了基于独立文档评分的排名方式在实现某些效用-公平性平衡时是次优的；实验证实半贪婪后处理方法在计算效率和权衡质量上优于传统的动态评分优化。

Conclusion: 评分函数在处理公平性与效用权衡时存在本质的次优性。相比之下，半贪婪后处理方法能够以可控的计算复杂度实现更优的权衡，接近穷举后处理的效果。

Abstract: Scoring functions are used to represent the relevance of individual documents. In modern information retrieval or recommendation systems, they are often learned from data and play a pivotal role in ranking sets of documents or items in a way that maximizes utility to a query or user. With the recent interest in algorithmic fairness, the success of scoring has naturally led to methods that learn scores that simultaneously trade off fairness and utility. In this work, we show that in stark contrast with utility-centric objectives, scoring is sub-optimal in achieving all utility-fairness trade-offs. We establish this with a series of counter-examples with a generic fairness formulation. We show that the issue persists whether we have a deterministic scoring function or a randomized one, or whether we measure fairness at the scope of a single query or across multiple queries. On the positive side, we empirically demonstrate that semi-greedy post-processing has the potential to achieve much better trade-offs, often approaching the ideal of exhaustive post-processing in a tractable way.

</details>


### [202] [TileMaxSim: IO-Aware GPU MaxSim Scoring with Dimension Tiling and Fused Product Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26439)
*Ashutosh Sharma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对 ColBERT 等多向量检索模型在 GPU 上的内存带宽瓶颈问题，TileMaxSim 通过 I/O 感知 Triton 内核技术（SRAM 分块、维度分块及融合 PQ），在不损失精度的前提下，实现了高达 80% 的内存带宽利用率，带来百倍级的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 ColBERT 类模型虽精度高，但其 GPU 实现效率低下。屋顶线分析（Roofline analysis）表明，这类算法因将相似度矩阵实例化到内存中，导致严重的内存带宽浪费（Naive 实现仅利用了峰值带宽的 5-18%）。

Method: 1. 多查询 SRAM 分块（Multi-query SRAM tiling）：将文档嵌入流式传输至共享内存并直接在寄存器中计算最大值，从而实现 HBM 的单次读取； 2. 维度分块（Dimension tiling）：支持对超过 128 维的嵌入进行分块处理； 3. 融合乘积量化（Fused PQ）评分：利用共享内存查询表大幅减少 HBM 的 I/O 开销。

Result: 在 NVIDIA H100 GPU 上，TileMaxSim 的 HBM 带宽利用率达到 80.2%，检索吞吐量提升至 82M 文档/秒，相比传统循环实现有 220 倍提升，相较于常见的 PyTorch 融合版本也有 6.5 倍以上的性能增益，并成功将 ColBERTv2 的检索延迟从 268ms 大幅降低至 1.2ms。

Conclusion: TileMaxSim 是一种高效的 I/O 感知型 Triton 内核，作为 ColBERTv2/PLAID 的直接替换方案，在保持完全相同检索质量的前提下，实现了极高的吞吐量并显著降低了端到端延迟，为大规模多向量检索提供了强大的底层算子支持。

Abstract: Multi-vector retrieval models such as ColBERT achieve state-of-the-art accuracy through fine-grained token-level MaxSim scoring, yet existing GPU implementations leave most hardware performance unused. We give a roofline analysis of MaxSim on modern GPUs and identify a severe bandwidth gap: naive implementations reach only 5-18% of peak HBM bandwidth because they materialize the Nq x Nd similarity matrix, wasting memory traffic on data that is consumed once and discarded. We present TileMaxSim, a family of IO-aware Triton kernels that close this gap via (1) multi-query SRAM tiling that streams document embeddings through shared memory while accumulating per-query-token maxima in registers, reading each embedding from HBM exactly once; (2) dimension tiling that partitions the embedding dimension into 128-wide chunks, enabling scoring for d > 128 embeddings that overflow shared memory; and (3) fused product-quantization scoring via shared-memory lookup tables, cutting HBM I/O by up to ~31x. On NVIDIA H100 GPUs, TileMaxSim reaches 80.2% of peak HBM bandwidth and scores 82M documents/second (71.6M/s on real MS MARCO passages), a 220x speedup over loop-based scoring, 6.5x over fused PyTorch, 6.6-8.5x over torch.compile, and 469x the scoring throughput of WARP's CPU engine on the same node. TileMaxSim preserves exact retrieval quality: on MS MARCO and three BEIR benchmarks, rankings match reference MaxSim. As a drop-in replacement in ColBERTv2/PLAID, it cuts scoring latency at 100K candidates from 268 ms to 1.2 ms (98% lower end-to-end latency). We further show constant throughput from 100K to 500K documents, data-parallel multi-GPU sharding, robustness across dimensions 64-768, and FP16/BF16/FP32 support. Concurrent work independently develops an IO-aware fused MaxSim kernel; we differ in dimension tiling for d > 128 and fused product-quantization scoring.

</details>


### [203] [GPUSparse: GPU-Accelerated Learned Sparse Retrieval with Parallel Inverted Indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26441)
*Ashutosh Sharma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GPUSparse 是一种高性能 GPU 加速的精确稀疏检索系统，通过优化倒排索引结构、批处理评分算法及 Triton 融合内核，在保持 SPLADE 精确检索效果的同时，将推理速度比传统 CPU 系统提升了数百倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 SPLADE 等学习型稀疏检索模型具有优秀的检索效果和可解释性，但其推理侧仍依赖于 CPU 计算受限的倒排索引遍历（如 WAND 算法），这成为了大规模实时检索的性能瓶颈。作者旨在开发一种 GPU 加速的系统以突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 1. 设计了 GPU 并发倒排索引，采用块对齐与 warp 合并的倒排表存储；2. 实现了批处理 scatter-add 评分算法，支持数百个查询并发处理；3. 开发了融合 Triton 内核，通过分析工作效率（work-efficiency）与硬件利用率（hardware utilization）之间的权衡，优化了内存带宽利用率。

Result: 在 MS MARCO (8.8M 文档) 上：1. 检索质量与 CPU 精确评分一致（MRR@10=0.383）；2. 相比 Pyserini CPU 速度提升 235 倍（1.27ms vs 298ms）；3. 相比最快的 CPU 稀疏检索系统 Seismic，在实现精确检索的同时达到 787 QPS 的吞吐量；4. 文档级并行内核达到了 H100 峰值 HBM 带宽的 62.6%。

Conclusion: GPUSparse 通过 GPU 加速实现了高性能的精确稀疏检索，证明了在保持高性能的同时，可以通过 GPU 核心优化（如合并内核、批处理 scatter-add）显著超越传统 CPU 倒排索引遍历的速度瓶颈，并揭示了 GPU 稀疏检索中工作效率与内存带宽利用率之间的权衡。

Abstract: Learned sparse retrieval models such as SPLADE achieve retrieval quality competitive with dense models while preserving the interpretability and exact-match advantages of sparse representations. However, inference-time scoring still relies on CPU-bound inverted index traversal algorithms (WAND, Block-Max WAND), creating a fundamental bottleneck for real-time serving at scale. We present GPUSparse, a system for GPU-accelerated exact learned sparse retrieval that introduces: (1) a GPU-parallel inverted index with block-aligned, warp-coalesced posting lists; (2) a batched scatter-add scoring algorithm that processes hundreds of queries simultaneously; and (3) fused Triton kernels with an analysis of the tradeoff between work-efficiency and hardware utilization. On MS MARCO passage ranking (8.8M passages) with real SPLADE embeddings, GPUSparse matches CPU exact scoring to three decimals (MRR@10=0.383, equal to Pyserini SPLADE at this precision; Recall@1000>=0.999 vs. dense matmul, the residual from floating-point tie-breaking) while providing a 235x speedup over Pyserini CPU at 8.8M documents (1.27ms vs. 298ms per query). Compared to Seismic (the fastest CPU sparse retrieval system), which trades 25% recall for speed (R@1000=0.738 vs. 0.983 exact), GPUSparse achieves exact scoring at 787 QPS throughput (batch 500) on the full 8.8M collection, with 1.3ms per query. Our document-parallel kernel reaches 62.6% of H100 peak HBM bandwidth, revealing a fundamental work-efficiency vs. bandwidth-efficiency tradeoff in GPU sparse retrieval. The reformulation of sparse scoring as scatter-add over an inverted index is shared with SPARe's iterative mode; our contribution is its fused-kernel realization, which we measure to be 23-270x faster than a faithful SPARe iterative reimplementation.

</details>


### [204] [Attributed, But Not Incremental: Cannibalization-Corrected Attribution for Large-Scale Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26690)
*Donghui Li,Bowen Yuan,Zili Yang,Qinxin Chen,Lijing Song*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对付费广告归因导致的高估增量问题，本文提出了一种结合增量实验与层级分配的修正框架，有效减少了广告对自然流量的蚕食。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模付费获取系统中，传统的归因方法往往高估了付费渠道的增量效应（蚕食了自然流量或品牌驱动流量），从而导致ROI测量失真及预算分配决策错误。

Method: 提出了一种实验校准的归因修正框架：1. 以增量实验（Incrementality Experiments）作为因果锚点，将稀疏的提升测量转化为每日修正估计；2. 在业务层级约束下，将校准后的蚕食量映射到生产层级的粒度。

Result: 离线验证显示，该方法相比原始归因和常规ML基线大幅降低了校准误差；在线部署于TikTok多个全球市场后，蚕食率降低了约15个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架有效校准了归因偏差，能够修正由于付费广告（如DNU）蚕食自然流量而导致的ROI夸大问题，在大规模生产环境中具有实用价值。

Abstract: In large-scale paid acquisition and growth advertising systems, production attribution outputs are widely used for daily budget allocation and channel diagnosis. However, paid-attributed conversions such as daily new users (DNU) may systematically overstate true incremental growth when paid channels overlap with organic demand, brand-driven traffic, or other acquisition channels. This attribution-cannibalization mismatch can distort incremental ROI measurement and budget decisions at scale.
  We propose an experiment-calibrated attribution correction framework that uses incrementality experiments as causal anchors to convert sparse lift measurements into daily correction estimates. To make the corrected signal actionable at production granularity, we further allocate calibrated cannibalization volume across business hierarchies under structural consistency constraints. Offline forward-in-time validation against channel-level incrementality experiment readouts shows that the proposed framework substantially reduces calibration error relative to raw attribution and fine-grained ML baselines. Deployed across multiple global TikTok markets, the system supported budget and traffic strategy adjustments that were followed by an approximately 15-percentage-point reduction in the measured cannibalization rate.

</details>


### [205] [A Shared IPTC Topic Space for Cross-Source Topic Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26845)
*Din Iskakov,Sebastian Gonçalves,Marco Idiat,Mendeli Vainstein,Aline Villavicencio,Ronaldo Menezes,Rodrigo Wilkens*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对跨媒体主题对比中模型空间不一致的痛点，本文提出了一种基于IPTC预定义分类法的引导式BERTopic框架，实现了跨语料库的可复制、统一化主题映射与比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术中，不同语料库的主题模型通常是独立训练的，导致主题空间不一致、无法直接横向对比，这阻碍了对跨媒体主题关注度的科学比较。

Method: 采用“引导式BERTopic”（Guided BERTopic）方法，利用IPTC媒体主题分类法作为外部锚点，通过加权关键词和目标质心（Target Centroids）进行评分，并将主题映射归纳至17个IPTC父类；开发过程包含模型初筛、映射精炼、决赛对比、消融实验及阈值校准等步骤。

Result: 在纽约时报2011年语料库的实验显示，引导式方法在严格的分配阈值下表现出比零样本基准更高的覆盖率；加入父类丰富的目标构建提升了覆盖率与分类一致性；模型在提高阈值时性能表现稳健。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一种基于外部标准（IPTC分类法）的共享主题空间构建框架，有效解决了跨语料库主题模型无法直接对比的难题，为跨媒体的主题研究提供了可复制的分析工具。

Abstract: Comparing topic attention across different media is hindered by a fundamental modelling problem: topic models fitted separately to each corpus produce corpus-specific topic spaces that cannot be aligned directly. This paper presents a reproducible framework that places corpora in a single shared topic space defined by a taxonomy. Discovered topics are obtained with guided BERTopic, scored against the ninety-four IPTC Media Topics' taxonomy topics (level-1) through weighted keyword and target centroids, and then collapsed upward to seventeen IPTC parent topics by a maximum-similarity rule. The framework was developed and selected on a controlled New York Times 2011 corpus through a narrowing sequence: a broad model screen, a focused mapping refinement, a strict finalist comparison, a target-construction ablation, and a threshold calibration. In this corpus, the guided family retained substantially stronger mapped coverage than a zero-shot benchmark under stricter assignment thresholds, a parent-enriched target construction improved both coverage and parent consistency, and coverage declined gradually rather than collapsing as the assignment threshold was tightened. The contribution is an externally anchored method for constructing a shared topic space that enables reproducible cross-source topic comparison.

</details>


### [206] [TriPAH: Imbalance-Aware Tri-Prompt Affinity Hashing for Cross-Modal Medical Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27010)
*Jiaming Bian,Songming Li,Yurui Song,Yunfei Chen,Yichao Cao,Jun Long*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TriPAH 是一种新型跨模态医学哈希检索框架，通过本体引导的提示学习与非对称多任务优化机制，解决了医学领域数据噪声和语义对齐难题，大幅提升了检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学跨模态哈希检索面临临床语言噪声大、标签分布长尾化以及量化过程导致语义对齐能力脆弱的问题，导致跨模态检索效率与精度不足。

Method: 提出 TriPAH 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于本体的提示合成（Ontology-grounded prompt synthesis）以获取低噪声文本表示；(2) 提示标记混合器（Prompt-token mixer）进行分层多粒度对齐；(3) 非对称多任务目标（Asymmetric multi-task objective），结合多正样本对比学习、不平衡感知分类及渐进式量化正则化，并辅以患者级一致性模块。

Result: 在三个公共数据集上的实验结果表明，TriPAH 在各项指标上均显著优于现有的前沿方法，验证了其在复杂医学数据环境下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TriPAH 框架通过引入本体引导的提示工程和多任务耦合优化，有效解决了跨模态医学哈希检索中语义对齐不足和量化损失的问题，在公开数据集上取得了优于现有前沿方法的结果。

Abstract: In the era of big medical data, efficient cross-modal retrieval is pivotal for evidence-based diagnosis and large-scale case management. Cross-modal medical hashing retrieval aims to enable efficient image-text search and support downstream tasks such as case-based reasoning and decision support by learning compact, semantically aligned binary codes. However, current methods suffer from semantic fragmentation due to noisy clinical language, long-tailed labels, and brittle quantization that weakens alignment. We propose TriPAH, a Tri-Prompt Affinity Hashing framework. TriPAH synthesizes ontology-grounded, patient-level prompts conditioned on normalized clinical cues to yield low-noise textual representations for initial alignment. A lightweight prompt-token mixer performs hierarchical, multi-granularity alignment and produces quantization-ready features under an asymmetric multi-task objective coupling multi-positive contrastive alignment, imbalance-aware classification, and progressive quantization regularization. A patient-level consistency module further stabilizes codes across complementary views. Extensive experiments on three public datasets demonstrate that TriPAH significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [207] [UniFormer: Efficient and Unified Model-Centric Scaling for Industrial Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27058)
*Bo Chen,Jinlong Jiao,Tijian Hu,Ruihao Zhang,Yanzhi Liu,Chenghou Jin,Qinglin Jia,Baixuan He,Hechang Pan,Yiwu Liu,Jian Liang,Chaoyi Ma,Ruiming Tang,Han Li,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对推荐系统模型扩展缺乏统一框架的问题，本文提出了 UniFormer，通过将建模空间解耦为特征和任务空间，并结合语义分词策略与多视图扩展机制，在工业级应用中实现了性能与推理效率的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐模型扩展方法多为组件级的独立扩展，或虽探索了跨模块协同扩展但局限于特征空间，缺乏统一的、全局的“模型中心化”缩放框架。

Method: 1. 空间分解：将模型空间划分为特征交互模块（Feature-space Interaction Modules）和任务交互模块（Task-space Interaction Modules）。2. 高效推理：引入基于语义的分词方案（Semantic-based tokenization）实现用户-物品解耦，支持请求级加速。3. 模式建模：利用多序列交叉注意力（Multi-sequence cross-attention）捕获异构行为模式，通过自注意力（Self-attention）增强交互建模。4. 灵活扩展：通过多视图 FFN（Multi-view FFNs）实现各建模组件的参数化灵活扩展。

Result: 在快手（Kuaishou）和快手极速版（Kuaishou Lite）的生产环境 A/B 测试中，UniFormer 显著提升了用户互动指标，其中 App 停留时长分别增长 0.101% / 0.260%，观看时长分别增长 0.729% / 1.113%。

Conclusion: UniFormer 是一种高效、统一的模型中心化扩展框架，通过任务与特征空间的解耦建模、语义分词机制以及多视图 FFN，在工业级推荐系统中平衡了性能提升与计算效率，验证了模型缩放范式在解决大规模推荐任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Recently, substantial progress has been made in industrial recommendation through component-centric model scaling, where individual components such as behavior modeling, feature interaction, or task modeling are independently scaled to improve model capacity. Although recent methods such as HyFormer and OneTrans further explore cross-module co-scaling by jointly modeling behavior and interaction, their designs are still confined to the feature space and lack a unified model-centric scaling framework over the overall modeling space. In this paper, we propose UniFormer, an efficient and unified model-centric scaling framework for industrial recommender systems. To improve efficiency, UniFormer decomposes the overall modeling space into feature and task spaces, which are modeled by stacked Feature-space Interaction Modules and Task-space Interaction Modules, respectively. Moreover, UniFormer introduces semantic-based tokenization scheme to enable user-item decoupling, thereby achieving request-level inference acceleration. To prevent preference collapse, UniFormer employs multi-sequence cross-attention to separately capture heterogeneous behavior patterns, followed by the self-attention to enhance interaction modeling. Besides, dedicated multi-view FFNs are introduced to support flexible and scalable parameter scaling across different modeling components. Extensive online A/B testing in two production scenarios, Kuaishou and Kuaishou Lite, shows that UniFormer consistently improves user engagement and interaction metrics, achieving gains of +0.101%/+0.260% in App Stay Time and +0.729%/+1.113% in Watch Time, respectively.

</details>


### [208] [TRUST: Item-Calibrated Interval Evidence for Temporal Session-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27214)
*Linjiang Guo,Nitin Bisht,Shiqing Wu,Yifan Yin,Guandong Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对现有会话推荐中“绝对时间间隔失效”的问题，本文提出了TRUST框架，通过根据各物品自身的间隔分布对时间信号进行个性化校准，显著提升了推荐准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于时间信号的会话推荐模型多依赖绝对时间间隔，忽略了不同物品之间时间间隔分布的显著差异，导致时间信号无法准确反映用户的真实兴趣。

Method: 提出TRUST框架，引入基于物品经验间隔分布的得分函数，对比绝对时间间隔，将该机制应用于全局邻居采样、会话图编码及兴趣聚合三个阶段。

Result: 在公开数据集上TRUST框架优于现有代表性基准模型，且作为插件能有效提升现有时间序列推荐模型性能；消融实验证实了在各模块中进行时间信号校准的有效性。

Conclusion: 针对物品进行个性化时间间隔校准（而非使用统一的绝对时间值）对于提升会话推荐效果至关重要，且该框架具有模型通用性。

Abstract: Temporal signals have been widely used in session-based recommendation to infer user interest. Existing temporal session-based recommenders primarily rely on absolute interval values, implicitly assuming that the same interval carries similar interest signals across items. However, we empirically find that this assumption does not hold: each item has its own interval distribution, so an interval should be interpreted relative to the item it belongs to. Based on this observation, we propose TRUST, a framework that evaluates each observed interval relative to the empirical interval distribution of the corresponding item. Specifically, we propose a score function to guide global neighbor sampling, session graph encoding, and final interest aggregation. Experiments on public datasets show that TRUST consistently improves over representative temporal and non-temporal baselines, and plug-in experiments further show that the proposed scoring function can improve existing temporal session recommenders as a model-agnostic method. Component-wise ablations further show that calibrating the temporal signals within each module, rather than removing the module itself, consistently improves neighbor sampling, session graph encoding, and interest aggregation.

</details>


### [209] [NOVA: A Verification-Aware Agent Harness for Architecture Evolution in Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27243)
*Shaohua Liu,Liang Fang,Yilong Sun,Shudong Huang,Qingsong Luo,Xiaoyang Chen,Dongqiang Liu,Chuangang Ma,Zhenzhen Chai,Henghuan Wang,Shijie Quan,Changyuan Cui,Zhangbin Zhu,Peng Chen,Wei Xu,Lei Xiao,Haijie Gu,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对工业推荐模型架构演进中自动化程度低、易产生静默失效的技术瓶颈，本文提出了NOVA代理框架，通过验证级联与层级任务控制，实现了高效、高质的自动化模型架构迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业广告推荐系统中，手动进行模型架构升级既耗时又依赖专家经验，而现有的AutoML仅限超参调优，通用LLM编码代理生成的架构常存在难以检测的“静默失效”问题，缺乏系统性的架构验证机制。

Method: 提出了NOVA（一个具备验证意识的架构演进代理框架），核心机制包括：1. 架构梯度（一种启发式非可导更新信号）；2. 验证级联（层层拦截无效架构）；3. L1-L4级任务控制（实现人机协同的风险管控）。

Result: NOVA在工业落地中表现优异：L2/L3任务通过率分别达到54.5%和60.0%，研发周期缩短超13倍；在线A/B测试中，多项pCVR目标的GMV显著提升（最高+2.02%），且模型偏差降低了37%-66%。

Conclusion: NOVA通过引入层级任务控制和验证级联，成功实现了工业级广告推荐模型的自动化架构演进，在显著提升研发效率的同时，保证了模型性能的提升与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Industrial advertising recommender models are continuously improved through architecture evolution. Upgrades such as RankMixer, TokenMixer-Large, and MixFormer show that better structures remain a key source of quality and business gains. Yet developing such upgrades in production is expert-intensive and difficult to scale. Existing automation is insufficient: AutoML mainly tunes hyper-parameters, while effective gains often require cross-module changes under strict constraints; generic LLM coding agents optimize for runnable code, but runnable code does not imply a valid recommender architecture. Candidates may pass local tests while causing silent failures that degrade performance.
  We present NOVA, a level-aware agent harness for verification-aware architecture evolution. NOVA uses an architecture gradient, an SGD-inspired, non-differentiable update signal that aggregates prior modifications, verification diagnostics, metric feedback, and trajectory memory to guide the next modification. A verification cascade checks structure semantics, local executability, offline effectiveness, and online impact; invalid candidates are blocked early, with failure patterns recorded as forbidden directions. L1--L4 task-level control matches automation to task complexity and risk, routing high-risk tasks to Copilot for human oversight. Deployed in an industrial advertising system, NOVA achieves the highest effective pass rate on L2 ScaleUp and L3 Literature-to-Production tasks (54.5% and 60.0%), reduces silent failures compared with coding-agent baselines, and shortens one literature-to-production cycle by over 13x in human-attended time. In online A/B testing, the selected L3 candidate improves GMV on three pCVR objectives by +1.25%, +1.70%, and +2.02%, while reducing pCVR bias by 58.8%, 66.7%, and 37.3%.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [Unsupervised Memory-Enhanced Video Transformers: Obstacle Detection for Autonomous Agricultural Rover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26151)
*Théo Biardeau,Anne-Sophie Capelle-Laizé,Salwan Alwan,David Helbert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于动态农业场景的无监督视频异常检测框架（VMTAD），通过引入记忆模块增强变压器的时序建模能力，有效解决了移动机器人在复杂环境下的障碍物检测及实时性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安防传感器（如LiDAR）在检测作物冠层下方障碍物时存在局限性；基于监督学习的视觉方法难以应对未见过的障碍物；现有的无监督异常检测方法在动态场景下效果不佳。因此，需要一种能在动态农业环境下进行实时、鲁棒的障碍物检测的方案。

Method: 提出了一种名为“视频记忆异常检测变压器”（VMTAD）的无监督学习方法。该方法采用基于变压器（Transformer）的架构，并集成了一个专用的记忆模块，旨在利用前序帧的编码表示来捕捉时间上下文信息，从而克服机器人运动引起的动态场景干扰。此外，该模型仅需在正常操作的图像上进行训练，无需任何标签。

Result: 在“Grillion”农业机器人及油菜数据集上进行了测试，检测任务的AUROC达到0.973，分割任务达到0.997；轻量化版本实现了14毫秒的实时推理速度，满足农业机器人的作业安全需求。

Conclusion: VMTAD 证明了基于变压器和记忆模块的无监督学习框架能够有效解决移动机器人（如农业漫游车）在动态场景下的障碍物检测难题，实现了高精度与实时性的平衡，提升了农业机器人的运行安全性。

Abstract: While autonomous rovers have become indispensable to precision farming, achieving consistent operational safety remains a critical challenge. Conventional safety sensors, such as LiDAR, fail to detect obstacles positioned below the plant canopy, posing a significant risk. While camera-based supervised learning methods can detect common objects, they perform poorly when faced with obstacles that were not present in their training data. Actual unsupervised anomaly detection offers a solution by learning the normal visual patterns of an environment, but often fails for the dynamic scenes captured by a moving rover.\\ This paper introduces Video Memory Transformers for Anomaly Detection (VMTAD), a fully unsupervised method designed for real-time obstacle detection in dynamic agricultural scenes. VMTAD utilizes a transformer-driven architecture augmented with a dedicated memory module. This memory module leverages temporal context by processing encoded representations of preceding frames. This approach enables the system to effectively address the dynamic context caused by the robot's movement. The model is trained using only images that represent normal operation, requiring no data labels.\\ VMTAD was rigorously evaluated on the 'Grillion' agricultural rover. On a challenging rapeseed dataset, VMTAD achieved state-of-the-art performance, reaching a 0.973 detection and 0.997 segmentation Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve. A lightweight variant provides an optimal balance of high accuracy and real-time inference (14 ms), which is critical for safety, as confirmed by our analysis of the rover's total stopping distance.

</details>


### [211] [Reinforcement Learning Enables Autonomous Microrobot Navigation and Intervention in Simulated Blood Capillaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26154)
*Jannik Drotleff,Samuel Tovey,Paul Hohenberger,Christoph Lohrmann,Julian Hoßbach,Konstantin Nikolaou,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过构建高保真毛细血管仿真，利用强化学习训练微型机器人掌握了复杂的导航策略，验证了其在生物体内进行精准医疗干预的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的微型机器人导航研究多局限于理想化环境，缺乏对血管内复杂流场、分支结构和细胞障碍的考虑，限制了其在实际医疗场景（如药物递送、血栓溶解）中的应用。

Method: 构建了包含真实流体动力学、红细胞动力学和解剖学分支结构的毛细血管网络仿真环境；利用深度强化学习训练微型机器人实现趋化性导航。

Result: 系统揭示了导航的物理限制（布朗运动与流动的影响），发现微型机器人自主形成了多种通用策略（如“运行-旋转”和“搜索-等待”），并能在无需重训练的情况下成功实现毛细血管的堵塞与疏通。

Conclusion: 强化学习（RL）是开发复杂生物环境中自主微型机器人干预策略的可行框架，即使在面对复杂血液动力学和障碍时也能发现有效的导航策略。

Abstract: Autonomous microrobots navigating biological vasculature could enable targeted drug delivery and thrombolysis, yet training control policies for realistic environments remains an open challenge. Prior reinforcement learning (RL) studies of microrobotic navigation have been limited to idealized geometries that omit complex hydrodynamic flow fields, confined branching structures, and dense cellular obstacles found in vivo. Here, we develop a physically grounded simulation of a blood capillary network, incorporating realistic hydrodynamic flow fields, explicit red blood cell dynamics, and anatomically derived branching geometry, and train deep RL agents to navigate it via chemotaxis. We systematically map the physical limits of navigation across robot size and swimming speed, revealing a forbidden regime where Brownian motion and flow overcome propulsion. Successful agents independently discover multiple universal strategy types, including run-and-rotate and energy-efficient search-and-sit policies, regardless of robot parameters. Without retraining, these agents perform targeted blocking and unblocking of capillary flow, restoring throughput to healthy baseline levels. These results establish RL as a viable framework for developing autonomous microrobotic intervention strategies in complex biological environments.

</details>


### [212] [RMTL: Reinforced Micro-task Learning for Long-Horizon Manipulation with VLM Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26175)
*Anıl Can Ateş,Orhan Kahraman,Cihan Topal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人长程操作中单一VLM奖励信号不敏感的问题，提出RMTL框架，通过分解任务为微任务、动态切换提示词及引入分层管理器，有效提升了学习效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）作为零样本奖励函数的方法，其单一的全局提示词（Global prompt）在长程复杂操作任务中过于笼统，导致奖励函数平滑且不敏感，难以引导智能体进行早期探索。

Method: 提出“强化微任务学习”（RMTL）框架：（1）将长程任务分解为一组语言描述的微任务；（2）使用多视角VLM奖励结合当前活动微任务的提示词计算奖励；（3）采用反向课程学习（Reverse curriculum）处理初始状态；（4）采用从基于规则的阶段切换演进为分层策略管理器（Hierarchical Manager）的方法进行训练。

Result: 在Fetch机器人操作环境中，与传统的单一提示词VLM奖励相比，RMTL提供了更具区分度的奖励信号，实现了更快的强化学习训练效率，且无需进行繁琐的提示词调优。

Conclusion: 将任务分解为语言描述的微任务（Micro-tasks）并结合分层学习策略，比使用单一全局提示词的VLM奖励更能提供信息丰富的反馈，从而显著提升机器人强化学习的收敛速度和对复杂环境的适应能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for robotic manipulation often requires manually designing a dense reward function, which is difficult to tune and often fragile, or learning a reward from human demonstrations or preferences, which can be expensive. A recent line of work uses pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) as zero-shot reward models, replacing these costs with a single text prompt. However, we argue that a single global prompt is too coarse for long-horizon manipulation tasks with randomized initial conditions. The single-prompt VLM reward is near-flat for much of the trajectory, making early progress hard for the agent to detect. We propose Reinforced Micro-Task Learning (RMTL), an approach that decomposes a manipulation task into a small set of language-described micro-tasks and trains the agent to switch between them. At each step, the agent receives a multi-view VLM reward computed using the prompt of the currently active micro-task and averaged across multiple camera views to reduce the effect of view-specific occlusions. A reverse curriculum gradually exposes the agent to harder initial conditions, while a PPO worker is first trained with a fixed distance-based rule that selects the active micro-task. We then replace this rule with a learned hierarchical manager, turning rule-based phase selection into a fully learned hierarchical policy. We instantiate RMTL on the Fetch manipulation environment using three short stage-specific prompts and without additional prompt tuning. Experiments show that RMTL provides more informative reward signals than single-prompt VLM rewards, enabling faster learning. These results suggest that decomposing VLM rewards into micro-task-specific language prompts can substantially improve the scalability of language-guided reinforcement learning for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [213] [LiMoDE: Rethinking Lifelong Robot Manipulation from a Mixture-of-Dynamic-Experts Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26183)
*Zhihao Gu,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了 LiMoDE，一种结合动态专家混合（MoE）与两阶段终身学习策略的新架构，通过动态组合异构专家实现机器人技能的高效迁移与连续任务适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调方法在多任务环境下难以有效提取和复用技能，且缺乏对技能间交互的模拟，导致在连续任务适应过程中存在灾难性遗忘。目标是构建一个能够利用先验知识进行连续任务适应的通用型机器人。

Method: 提出 LiMoDE（终身动态专家混合模型），包含两个阶段：1. 多任务预训练阶段：利用基于运动信息激活的动态 MoE 架构学习通用先验知识；2. 任务适应阶段：通过终身 MoE 适应机制，动态组合新任务专家与冻结的预训练专家，实现知识迁移。

Result: 在仿真终身学习基准测试和真实世界机器人操作任务中表现优异，实现了出色的终身适应性能，且仅引入了适度的额外参数和推理负担。

Conclusion: LiMoDE 框架通过动态 MoE 架构和终身适应机制，有效解决了机器人操作在终身学习过程中的知识复用与灾难性遗忘问题，实现了高性能的跨任务适应。

Abstract: Building a generalist robot that can leverage prior knowledge for continuous task adaptation remains a significant challenge. Previous works alleviate the catastrophic forgetting problem by parameter-efficient fine-tuning for single-task adaptation.
