While recent data-driven policies show promise, their reliance on isolated contact points or latent affordance embeddings lacks the rigorous kinematic constraints necessary for complex articulated objects.To overcome the limitation, we introduce RelAfford6D, a novel training-free framework centered on a Relational 6D Affordance Graph. Given a free-form instruction, our system deduces a semantic topology linking a primary interacting part to its physical anchor. By elevating these topological nodes into precise metric $SE(3)$ poses via vision foundation models, we analytically formulate downstream execution as a kinematic constraint satisfaction problem. The robot synthesizes continuous trajectories by tracking strictly defined physical manifolds (e.g., revolute or prismatic orbits). Coupled with a closed-loop tracking mechanism for dynamic replanning against disturbances, our physically grounded approach achieves superior zero-shot success rates, cross-category generalization and execution robustness in both simulation and the real world environments, outperforming existing data-driven baselines.

</details>


### [249] [ForesightSafety-VLA: A Unified Diagnostic Safety Benchmark for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27079)
*Mingyang Lyu,Yinqian Sun,Yiyang Jia,Sicheng Shen,Moquan Sha,Huangrui Li,Feifei Zhao,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对VLA模型的具身安全性，提出了ForesightSafety-VLA基准测试，通过多维度的分类体系与细粒度的评价指标，诊断发现当前VLA模型在复杂场景与视觉扰动下表现出明显的不安全性，强调了安全需集成于基础能力的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在通用任务能力上不断进步，但其在物理世界的具身安全性尚未得到充分探索和理解。需要从整体视角对VLA模型的安全能力进行定性与定量的诊断。

Method: 提出了ForesightSafety-VLA诊断基准，构建了包含13种安全类别的分类体系（涵盖物理交互、指令、感知三个维度）；通过对场景结构、语言指令、视觉信息三方面的受控变分进行评估；引入累计安全成本(CC)和风险暴露时间(RET)等度量指标，并结合四象限分解法分析成功与失败案例。

Result: 在RoboTwin模拟环境中使用5种具身形态对代表性VLA基线进行测试。结果显示，即便最强的模型也存在显著的安全成本和不安全执行成功，且结构和视觉层面的变分对安全性的负面影响远大于语言层面的变分。

Conclusion: 具身智能的安全性与感知、定位和控制能力紧密耦合，无法仅通过后置安全过滤（post-hoc filtering）解决。实验证明模型在场景结构和视觉变化下表现出明显的安全性退化。

Abstract: In embodied intelligence, safety is a prerequisite for reliable robot deployment in the physical world. Current vision-language-action (VLA) models continue to advance toward general-purpose task capability, yet their embodied safety limits remain poorly understood. To address this gap, we introduce ForesightSafety-VLA, a diagnostic benchmark that makes safety the primary evaluation target for VLA systems. We define a 13-category safety taxonomy covering physical interaction safety (Safe-Core), instruction-side safety (Safe-Lang), and perception-side safety (Safe-Vis), and evaluate policies under three controlled dimensions of variation -- scene structure, language command, and visual observation -- so that failure sources can be diagnosed rather than hidden in a single aggregate score. Beyond binary task success, ForesightSafety-VLA measures process-level risk through cumulative safety cost (CC) and risk exposure time (RET), together with a four-quadrant decomposition of safe/unsafe success and failure. We instantiate 66 safety-augmented base scenarios in RoboTwin across 5 embodiments and report results on representative VLA baselines. Across the evaluated baselines, even the strongest policy incurs non-trivial safety cost and unsafe nominal success, while structure and visual variation induce substantially stronger safety degradation than ordinary language variation. These results suggest that embodied safety is tightly coupled to perception, grounding, and control competence rather than being reducible to post-hoc safety filtering alone.

</details>


### [250] [Proposal-Conditioned Latent Diffusion for Closed-Loop Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27123)
*Shubham Vaijanath Phoolari,Aleyna Kara,Christoph Lauer,Steven Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效的扩散模型交通仿真框架，通过动作压缩和提案初始化提升了采样速度，并利用测试时引导增强了场景的可控性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的闭环交通仿真在生成场景一致性和可控性交互行为的同时，面临高计算成本问题，限制了其在自动驾驶规划和实时模拟中的应用。

Method: 提出了一种基于实例中心场景上下文和多模态提案先验的扩散模型框架；引入了紧凑的动作潜空间（action-latent）表示和基于提案的初始化方法，并结合测试时引导（test-time guidance）来调控安全关键行为。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上的实验表明，该方法在真实性、安全性和可控性之间取得了良好平衡，同时通过测试时引导实现了对竞争目标的系统化权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于紧凑动作潜空间表示和提案引导的扩散模型框架，能够在保持生成真实性和安全性的同时，显著提升交互式交通仿真的计算效率，并支持通过测试时引导实现多目标平衡。

Abstract: Closed-loop traffic simulation remains challenging because it must generate interactive multi-agent behaviors that are scene-consistent and controllable throughout rollout. Prior diffusion-based approaches achieve strong realism, but their computational cost can hinder deployment in time-constrained replanning loops for autonomous vehicle planning and simulation. We present a diffusion-based scenario generation framework conditioned on instance-centric scene context and multimodal proposal priors, with optional test-time guidance for shaping safety-critical behaviors. A compact action-latent representation and proposal-based initialization improve sampling efficiency and reduce per-step runtime without retraining. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset demonstrate a favorable balance among realism, safety, and controllability across diverse interactive scenarios, while showing that test-time guidance enables systematic trade-offs among competing objectives.

</details>


### [251] [PAMAE: Phase-Aware-MoE Action Experts Towards Reliable Flow-Matching Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27144)
*Jiayu Yang,Tao Yang,Xiang Chang,Fei Chao,Changjing Shang,Qiang Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAMAE 是一种为流匹配 VLA 模型设计的“即插即用”混合专家模块，通过阶段感知路由实现多阶段操作任务中的动作专家动态分配，显著提升了机器人任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配 VLA 模型通常依赖单一的共享动作专家，难以捕捉多阶段任务中各阶段特有的控制模式，导致模型在复杂操作中的可靠性不足。

Method: 1. 提出 PAMAE 模块，用稀疏专家混合架构替换原有单一动作专家；2. 设计阶段感知路由（Phase-Aware Router），利用阶段预测头获取执行阶段线索；3. 采用两阶段训练方案：先进行基础动作学习预热，再通过路由对齐目标优化阶段一致性路由策略。

Result: 在多阶段机器人操作模拟任务中，PAMAE 相比强 VLA 基线模型将任务成功率提升了最高 9.2%，且各核心设计组件均被验证对性能提升至关重要。

Conclusion: PAMAE 通过引入阶段感知（Phase-Aware）的专家混合架构，证明了基于执行阶段的动作专家分配能够有效提升流匹配（flow-matching）VLA 模型在多阶段机器人操作任务中的可靠性和动作质量。

Abstract: Reliable action generation for multi-stage robotic manipulation remains challenging for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. While existing flow-matching VLA policies offer strong multimodal grounding and generalization, they typically employ a single shared action expert, limiting their ability to capture phase-specific control patterns across distinct execution stages. We propose a plug-and-play Phase-Aware Mixture-of-Experts Action Module (PAMAE), as a step towards more reliable phase-consistent action generation. PAMAE replaces the original flow-matching action expert with a sparse expert mixture while preserving the pretrained VLA backbone. PAMAE introduces a phase-aware router that leverages execution-phase cues to allocate action generation across experts, supported by a lightweight phase prediction head and a routing alignment objective. To stabilize specialization, we adopt a two-stage training scheme that first warms up the expert module under the standard flow-matching loss and then optimizes phase-consistent routing under auxiliary supervision. On multi-stage manipulation simulation tasks, PAMAE improves task success by up to \textbf{9.2\%} over strong VLA baselines. Further ablations show that both phase-supervised routing and staged optimization are essential for the observed gains. Our results highlight phase-consistent expert allocation as an effective mechanism for improving the reliability and action quality of flow-matching VLA policies.

</details>


### [252] [PhysReflect-VLA: Physical Feasibility and Self-Reflective Regulation for Reliable Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27146)
*Jiayu Yang,Tao Yang,Weijun Li,Xiang Chang,Fei Chao,Changjing Shang,Qiang Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysReflect-VLA 是一种为 VLA 模型设计的即插即用框架，通过引入物理可行性核查与 LLM 驱动的在线自我反思机制，显著提高了长程机器人操作的稳定性和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型通常采用前馈方式生成动作，缺乏对物理可行性的检查及执行过程中的在线自纠错能力，导致长程任务中容易出现因接触引起的扰动或不可行转换而失败的问题。

Method: 提出 PhysReflect-VLA 框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 可行性算子（Feasibility Operator），用于评估动作的动力学一致性；2) 动作解释算子（Action Explanation Operator），用于验证转换连贯性；3) 基于 LLM 的反思模块，用于分析状态偏差并生成纠正指导。采用两阶段训练过程，将这些模块整合至闭环控制管道中。

Result: 在多阶段、接触密集的真实世界操作任务中，该方法显著提升了阶段性稳定性和整体成功率，相比基准方案平均提升了 5.4%。消融实验证实了可行性检查与反思纠正机制对鲁棒执行的贡献。

Conclusion: 在长程机器人操作任务中，嵌入物理一致性检查和在线自我反思机制对于提升执行可靠性至关重要。PhysicalReflect-VLA 为解决 VLA 模型在物理可行性和误差纠正方面的不足提供了有效的框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation is highly sensitive to physically infeasible transitions, contact-induced disturbances, and the lack of effective self-correction during execution. Although Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide strong task grounding through multimodal learning, they typically generate actions in a feed-forward manner without explicitly checking physical feasibility or diagnosing execution errors online. We present PhysReflect-VLA, a plug-and-play execution-time reliability framework that augments VLA policies with physical feasibility evaluation and structured self-reflection in a closed-loop control pipeline. A Feasibility Operator evaluates whether candidate actions induce dynamically consistent state transitions; an Action Explanation Operator verifies transition coherence; and an LLM-based Reflection Module analyzes state discrepancies to generate corrective guidance for subsequent actions. A two-stage training procedure stabilizes feasibility modeling and integrates reflection into the control loop. Experiments on multi-stage, contact-rich real-world manipulation tasks show consistent improvements in stage-wise stability and overall task success compared with representative VLA baselines with an average gain of 5.4\%. Ablation results further indicate that feasibility checking and reflection-based correction both contribute to improved execution robustness. These results highlight the importance of embedding physical consistency checks and online self-reflection for reliable long-horizon robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [253] [Learning to Fold: prizewinning solution at LeHome Challenge 2026 (1st place online, 2nd offline)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27163)
*Ilia Larchenko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于VLA模型的强化学习框架，通过让策略网络自身评估任务进度与成功率来进行自我改进，在双臂衣物折叠任务中表现优异，于ICRA 2026竞赛中获仿真赛第一、实机赛第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对ICRA 2026“LeHome Challenge”竞赛，旨在解决双臂机器人进行衣物折叠任务的复杂操作问题，并探索如何高效提升视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的性能。

Method: 1. 核心架构：利用同一VLA网络同时预测动作、任务成功率及进度，作为价值评估依据（Advantage Estimation）；
2. 算法集成：结合AWR与RECAP实现流动匹配VLA模型；
3. 工程流水线：采用基于HuggingFace Hub的异步分布式训练及推演；
4. 部署与优化：利用Thompson采样进行推理时超参数优化；
5. Sim-to-Real策略：包含相机校准工具、强数据增强及DAgger式的人机协作（HIL）数据收集。

Result: 在62支参赛队伍中，该系统在在线仿真阶段获得第1名，在真实世界决赛中获得第2名。

Conclusion: 该系统通过将现有强化学习理念与工程优化（如流动匹配VLA、异步分布式训练及推理时参数优化）相结合，证明了即使在复杂的双臂衣物折叠任务中，通过VLA策略自身的价值评估也能实现高效的策略改进。

Abstract: I describe my solution to the LeHome Challenge 2026, an ICRA 2026 competition on bimanual garment folding. The system placed 1st of 62 teams in the online (simulation) round and 2nd in the real-world final. It improves a vision-language-action (VLA) policy with a reinforcement-learning loop. The policy is its own value function: the same network that predicts actions also predicts success, progress, and a few task-relevant future quantities, and those predictions drive advantage estimation, live failure detection, and candidate selection. The work mostly recombines existing RL ideas with engineering and optimization contributions that can be used together as one recipe or individually: AWR + RECAP combined for flow-matching VLA; an asynchronous distributed training / rollout pipeline through HuggingFace Hub; inference-time hyperparameters optimization via Thompson sampling; a sim-to-real recipe with camera-alignment tooling, heavy augmentation and DAgger-like HIL data collection.

</details>


### [254] [HumanoidUMI: Bridging Robot-Free Demonstrations and Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27239)
*Hongwu Wang,Chenhao Yu,Youhao Hu,Jiachen Zhang,Yuanyuan Li,Shaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 HumanoidUMI，一种低成本的人形机器人全身动作数据采集框架，通过 VR 设备与 UMI 抓手获取人类演示，结合全身控制策略实现复杂技能的学习与迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人技能学习极度依赖高质量全身示范数据，但现有的机器人远程操控方案存在硬件依赖强、门槛高、效率低下等问题，缺乏一种简单、灵活的获取全身数据的方法。

Method: 提出 HumanoidUMI 框架，利用轻量化 VR 设备和类 UMI（Universal Manipulation Interface）手柄采集人类关键点轨迹、腕部视角观测及末端动作；训练高层策略预测关键点，并通过全身控制器的运动重定向（Retargeting）机制完成机器人全身动作执行。

Result: 在五种真实世界场景中验证了该框架的有效性，成功实现了人形机器人全身技能的迁移学习，证明了采集到的演示数据对复杂行为学习具有高质量支持。

Conclusion: HumanoidUMI 成功实现了一种便携、低成本且无需实体的机器人全身技能数据采集范式，证明了基于人类演示进行全身控制策略训练的可行性与高效性，为人形机器人技能学习提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: High-quality demonstration data are essential for humanoid robot skill learning, especially for whole-body behaviors that require coordinated perception, locomotion, and manipulation. Existing data-collection methods largely rely on robot teleoperation, which is constrained by hardware accessibility, operator expertise, and limited efficiency. Inspired by the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI), we propose HumanoidUMI, a portable and robot-free framework for humanoid whole-body data collection. HumanoidUMI uses lightweight VR devices and UMI-inspired grippers to collect sparse human keypoint trajectories, wrist-view observations, and gripper actions. These demonstrations train a high-level policy to predict future keypoints, which are retargeted to robot-native whole-body references and executed by a whole-body controller. Experiments in five real-world scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework and validate the collected demonstrations for transferable humanoid whole-body skill learning.

</details>


### [255] [Advancing Omnimodal Embodied Agents from Isolated Skills to Everyday Physical Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27251)
*Junhao Shi,Zezheng Huai,Siyin Wang,Jia Chen,Yubang Wang,Zhaoye Fei,Hechang Chen,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniAct是一个用于持久化具身智能代理的异步分层框架，通过整合统一的语义规划、事件压缩内存和异步执行验证，解决了系统在复杂环境下的扩展性、上下文管理及鲁棒性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理系统在处理非结构化环境的长期任务时，因缺乏统一的网络-物理动作空间、上下文增长导致的性能衰退以及缺乏故障闭环机制，难以实现持续稳定运行。

Method: 提出OmniAct框架，包含：1. 多模态语义规划器（统一动作空间）；2. 自适应分层内存（利用事件边界压缩实现亚线性增长）；3. 异步视觉抢占引擎（实现物理执行中的闭环验证）。

Result: 在40项实际长周期任务中显著提升成功率，在超过10万个交互Token下保持近乎恒定的计算消耗，并使中等规模开源模型达到了闭源模型的性能水平。

Conclusion: 通过分层异步架构有效解决了长期自主代理在网络与物理空间协同、内存管理及执行验证方面的痛点，证明了该结构在扩展性和鲁棒性上的优势。

Abstract: Building persistent embodied agents in unstructured environments demands unified orchestration of heterogeneous tools spanning both cyber (APIs, IoT) and physical (manipulation, navigation) domains, coupled with autonomous recovery from physical failures that inevitably arise over extended operation. Existing systems treat these as separate problems: VLM-based planners lack a unified cyber-physical action space, agent frameworks accumulate unbounded context that degrades temporal coherence, and VLA policies execute open-loop without detecting their own failures. We argue that persistent autonomy requires not a monolithic model but a hierarchical asynchronous architecture with explicit separation of planning, memory, and verification. To this end, we present OmniAct, a framework integrating a multimodal semantic planner for skill routing across unified action spaces, an adaptive hierarchical memory with event-boundary-driven compression for sub-linear context growth, and an asynchronous visual preemption engine that closes the semantic loop during physical execution. Across 40 real-world long-horizon tasks on two robotic platforms coordinating four IoT devices, OmniAct achieves consistent improvements in end-to-end success across all complexity levels, maintains near-flat token consumption over under 100k+ accumulated interaction tokens, and elevates mid-scale open-weight models to proprietary-level performance.

</details>


### [256] [E-TTS: A New Embodied Test-Time Scaling Framework for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27268)
*Wen Ye,Peiyan Li,Tingyu Yuan,Yuan Xu,Xiangnan Wu,Chaoyang Zhao,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: E-TTS 是一个即插即用的具身测试时扩展框架，通过融合历史感知的迭代推理与行动评估机制，在无需额外训练的前提下，显著增强了机器人的长程决策能力与任务完成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能测试时扩展（TTS）研究存在两个局限：缺乏对推理扩展机制的深入研究，且现有方法难以通过长跨度的历史上下文有效辅助行动决策。

Method: E-TTS 框架：引入“历史感知迭代优化”（History-aware Iterative Refinement），通过推理-行动联合采样、基于历史缓存（History Buffer）的视觉语言验证器（VLM Verifiers）进行评估，并构建了闭环反馈生成机制，实现模块化、插件化的测试时扩展。

Result: 在4个基准、6个环境、3种具身形态及4种基础视觉-语言-动作模型下进行验证，仿真环境性能提升达33.14%，真实世界场景提升达26.62%。

Conclusion: E-TTS 通过结合推理与行动的迭代优化机制，有效解决了具身智能任务中长序列历史上下文缺失及推理扩展机制不明的问题，在无需重训练的情况下显著提升了机器人操作性能。

Abstract: Recently, a few works have made early attempts to study test-time scaling for embodied tasks. However, two major challenges remain unsolved: (1) reasoning can effectively improve the performance of the policy, but its scaling mechanism has seldom been studied; (2) historical information is essential, as embodied tasks are inherently long-horizon and sequential, making sole reliance on current observations for action scaling inadequate due to the lack of historical context utilization. To address these challenges, we introduce E-TTS, a modular and plug-and-play Embodied Test-Time Scaling framework that unifies reasoning and action scaling for robotic manipulation via history-aware iterative refinement with vision-language verifiers. To support joint reasoning-action scaling, E-TTS performs reasoning-action joint sampling and scoring in a pairwise manner. To better utilize historical information, E-TTS uses a history buffer to store historical context, which is then used by reasoning and action verifiers to evaluate the sampled candidates. Unlike conventional open-loop TTS methods, E-TTS introduces feedback generation into the sampling process to form a closed-loop iterative refinement mechanism, enhancing both inference efficiency and environmental adaptability. Each component functions as an independent and composable module, allowing flexible and adaptive configuration depending on task requirements. To evaluate the advantages of our framework, we conduct experiments across 4 different benchmarks, 6 environments, 3 embodiments, and 4 base vision-language-action models. The experimental results demonstrate that, without requiring additional expert data collection or retraining, E-TTS consistently improves performance, achieving up to a 33.14% increase in simulation and 26.62% in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [257] [BOWConnect: Parallel Bayesian Optimization over Windows with Learned Local Cost Maps for Sample-Efficient Kinodynamic Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27292)
*Sourav Raxit,Abdullah Al Redwan Newaz,Jose Fuentes,Leonardo Bobadilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BOWConnect 是一种新型双向并行运动规划器，结合贝叶斯优化与动态规划策略，显著提升了高维非凸复杂空间中的规划效率、成功率及实时避障性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于采样的运动规划方法在高维状态空间采样效率低、动态约束下的代价启发式函数不可靠，且在狭窄通道等复杂环境中表现不佳。

Method: 提出 BOWConnect 方法：1. 在并行树探索框架中引入“窗口贝叶斯优化”（BOW）作为学习型转向函数；2. 采用双向树搜索（从起点和终点同时生长）；3. 利用空间哈希机制加速连接查询；4. 使用边值问题求解器生成满足动力学约束的轨迹。

Result: 在十个基准测试中实现了 100% 的成功率，并在复杂场景下保持了最优或准最优的规划时间；在实体地面车辆和四旋翼飞行器上验证了其实时无碰撞规划能力。

Conclusion: BOWConnect 是一个高效的运动规划框架，通过贝叶斯优化学习与双向路径规划的结合，有效解决了高维空间动力学约束规划中的效率与性能瓶颈，在复杂环境下表现出优越的鲁棒性和实时性。

Abstract: This paper presents BOWConnect, a bidirectional parallel kinodynamic motion planner that addresses three fundamental limitations of existing sampling-based methods: sample inefficiency in high-dimensional state spaces, unreliable cost heuristics under dynamic constraints, and poor performance in narrow passage environments. Unlike classical planners that rely on random control sampling and geometric distance heuristics, BOWConnect integrates Bayesian Optimization over Windows (BOW) as a learning-based steering function within a parallel tree-based exploration framework, enabling each worker to learn local cost maps and constraints to guide sampling toward dynamically feasible and collision-free controls. A bidirectional architecture simultaneously grows forward and backward trees from the start and goal regions in parallel threads, with a spatial hashing mechanism enabling fast connection queries and a boundary value problem solver generating kinodynamically consistent bridge trajectories. Extensive evaluations across ten benchmark environments demonstrate that BOWConnect achieves 100\% success while delivering the fastest or near-fastest planning time in complex scenarios, including narrow passages and non-convex spaces where state-of-the-art planners fail or degrade substantially. Real-world deployment on a ground vehicle and a quadrotor confirms real-time planning with no collisions. Videos of real-world and simulated experiments, high-resolution versions of the figures, and the open-source code are available at https://bow-connect.github.io/.

</details>


### [258] [LA4VLA: Learning to Act without Seeing via Language-Action Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27295)
*Tao Lin,Yuxin Du,Yiran Mao,Zewei Ye,Yilei Zhong,Bing Cheng,Yiming Wang,Jiting Liu,Yang Tian,Junchi Yan,Feiran Wu,Zenan Meng,Hu Wei,Yuqian Fu,Gen Li,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对 VLA 模型过度依赖视觉信号导致语言指令理解力差的问题，本文提出了 LA4VLA 框架。通过构建语言-动作数据集并在训练中引入语言-动作先验，有效增强了机器人的泛化能力与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型训练中，密集的视觉信号往往掩盖了稀疏的语言信号，导致模型产生“视觉捷径”（visual shortcuts），从而对视觉变化敏感，难以真正理解语言指令对动作的约束作用。

Method: 1. 提出了 LA4VLA 框架：通过将演示轨迹分解为原子动作并配对低级语言描述，构建了 LA4-33K 数据集，实现无需视觉输入的语言-动作先验学习。
2. 开发了 1B 参数的轻量级 VLA 模型 LA4VLA-1B。
3. 探索了三种融合策略：纯 LA 预训练、顺序 LA 到 VLA 迁移、混合 LA-VLA 预训练。

Result: 混合 LA-VLA 预训练表现最优。在模拟和真实世界任务中，相比无预训练基准，LA4VLA-1B 的任务成功率分别提高了 17.8% 和 45.0%。

Conclusion: LA4VLA 提供了一种在 VLA 模型中加入语言-动作预训练的有效策略，能够显著提升模型对语言指令的遵循能力，减轻对视觉“捷径”的依赖，最终显著提高在模拟及真实机器人场景下的任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are commonly pretrained on robot demonstrations by jointly mapping visual observations and language instructions to actions. However, dense visual-action supervision can dominate the comparatively sparse language-action signal. As a result, policies may rely on visual shortcuts rather than learn how language conditions action execution, making them sensitive to visual variations. To address this limitation, we propose LA4VLA, a language-action pretraining framework that enables policies to acquire language-conditioned action priors without visual observations. These priors capture reusable manipulation skills shared across tasks and scenes, reducing reliance on scene-specific visual cues. Specifically, LA4VLA decomposes expert demonstration trajectories into atomic action segments and pairs each segment with a corresponding low-level action description. This yields LA4-33K, a dataset of 33K Language-Action (LA) episodes derived entirely from existing demonstrations without additional robot data collection. We further develop LA4VLA-1B, a lightweight 1B-parameter VLA model, and investigate three paradigms for incorporating language-action supervision into VLA learning: LA-only pretraining, sequential LA-to-VLA pretraining, and mixed LA-VLA pretraining. Across simulation and real-world tasks, LA-pretrained policies consistently outperform matched VLA-pretrained counterparts, while combining LA and VLA supervision leads to further gains. In particular, mixed LA-VLA pretraining improves the average success rate of LA4VLA-1B over the no-pretraining baseline by up to 17.8 and 45.0 percentage points in simulation and real-world tasks, respectively. These results establish LA4VLA as an effective and complementary pretraining strategy for building stronger and more robust VLA policies.

</details>


### [259] [VibeAct: Vibration to Actions for Contact-Rich Reactive Robot Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27344)
*Yuemin Mao,Uksang Yoo,Jean Oh,Jonathan Francis,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VibeAct 提出了一种通过压电麦克风获取振动触觉的方法，利用自动标注将触觉信号抽象为“接触/滑移”表征，成功实现了触觉增强型灵巧手操作策略在仿真中的训练及向现实硬件的高效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手操作高度依赖快速、局部的接触事件，而压电麦克风虽然能提供高带宽特征，但其原始信号难以在高保真仿真中生成，导致 Sim-to-Real 的强化学习策略难以有效利用这些触觉信息。

Method: 1. 硬件集成：在灵巧手集成压电麦克风采集振动信号；2. 自动标注：通过在数字孪生体中重放录音来自动标注接触与滑移状态；3. 触觉估计模型：训练模型从原始波形中提取接触与滑移特征；4. 解耦强化学习：策略在仿真中通过这些高层抽象特征进行训练，避免了对原始音频的直接模拟。

Result: 在五项涉及重抓取、转动和插入的复杂任务中，VibeAct 显著优于基线方案（本体感知+点云），特别是在需要持续闭环反应的任务中表现卓越，且在真实灵巧手硬件上验证了策略的有效迁移。

Conclusion: VibeAct 成功解决了高频振动触觉传感在模拟与现实（Sim-to-Real）中的衔接难题。通过解耦感知与决策，该方法使得机器人能够利用精细的触觉（特别是滑移信息）处理复杂的操作任务，并实现了从模拟训练到物理平台的有效迁移。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation depends on contact events that are fast, local, and often visually occluded. Piezoelectric microphones offer a compact and high-bandwidth way to sense these interactions, but the resulting vibro-acoustic signals are difficult to simulate faithfully enough for end-to-end sim-to-real policy learning on dexterous robot hands. We propose VibeAct, a framework that bridges real vibrotactile sensing and simulation-based reinforcement learning through a shared physical representation of contact and slip. In the real world, we embed piezoelectric microphones into a dexterous robot hand and collect vibro-acoustic data through teleoperation, then replay the recordings in a calibrated digital clone to automatically label per-finger contact and slip. A tactile estimator learns to predict contact and slip from real microphone waveforms, while manipulation policies are trained in simulation on the same representation computed directly from simulated contacts. This decoupling lets policies exploit rapid tactile feedback without simulating raw audio. Across five contact-rich tasks spanning regrasping, in-hand reorientation, and insertion, VibeAct consistently outperforms a proprioception-and-point-cloud baseline in simulation, with the largest gains on tasks requiring sustained reactive control, where the continuous slip-magnitude channel proves the most informative observation. The learned policies transfer to a physical dexterous hand-arm platform, improving success rates on deployed tasks. Project videos and additional details are at https://vibeact.github.io/.

</details>


### [260] [Bridging Performance and Generalization in Reinforcement Learning for Agile Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27348)
*Jonathan Green,Jiaxu Xing,Nico Messikommer,Angel Romero,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出了一种自动无人机零样本泛化竞速框架，通过结合任务感知切换与物理驱动的程化赛道生成，在实现7.4倍泛化性能提升的同时，保持了高质量的竞速速度，并成功在视觉感知竞速中落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的自动无人机竞速强化学习模型在特定赛道表现优秀，但缺乏泛化能力，难以适应新环境；现有的泛化提升方法通常会导致严重的性能和速度下降，该研究旨在解决这一“性能与泛化”之间的矛盾。

Method: 提出了一种基于强化学习的无人机竞速框架，结合了基于学习进度的“任务感知切换”（task-aware switching）机制与“物理驱动的过程化赛道生成器”（physically informed procedural track generator），实现了无需测试时适应的零样本泛化。

Result: 该方法在真实世界的未知赛道上实现了零样本高性能飞行，泛化能力较现有技术提升了7.4倍，并在端到端的视觉控制设置（无需显式状态估计）中展现出了以往方法无法比拟的稳健性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了自动无人机竞速中零样本泛化的难题，在不牺牲速度的前提下，实现了强泛化能力的策略；其方法通过任务感知的切换策略与物理驱动的程序化生成，极大地增强了无人机在未见环境中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous drone racing is a fundamentally challenging regime for autonomous aerial robots, requiring time-optimal control while operating under persistent actuation saturation. While reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved human-level performance in this domain, current methods fail to generalize; policies trained on specific environments often crash immediately in unseen configurations. This failure reflects the intrinsic difficulty of zero-shot generalization in agile flight, arising from high-dimensional task variation and the tight coupling between safety and performance at high speeds. Existing approaches that improve generalization impose a substantial cost on flight speed: control policies must significantly degrade performance to achieve even modest levels of generalization. In this work, we propose a framework for zero-shot generalization in agile flight for RL-based drone racing. By combining task-aware switching based on learning progress with a physically informed procedural track generator, the framework produces a fast and robust generalist policy without test-time adaptation. Our method achieves strong zero-shot performance across a wide range of unseen racetracks in the real world, demonstrating a 7.4x improvement in generalization over the state-of-the-art approaches, while maintaining competitive racing speeds. We validate our method's results in both simulation and real-world settings, including a challenging vision-based, end-to-end control setting that operates without explicit state estimation, where all prior approaches fail to generalize.

</details>


### [261] [Continual Robot Policy Learning via Variational Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27353)
*Jiaxu Xing,Zhiyuan Zhu,Yunfan Ren,Ismail Geles,Yifan Zhai,Rudolf Reiter,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使机器人能适应多变、循环真实环境动力学的连续学习框架，通过在线推断隐变量来快速调节策略以应对环境扰动，显著提升了控制任务的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的机器人控制器通常在单次训练后固定，无法适应真实环境中随时间改变的各种动力学因素（如风力、负载变化、硬件磨损等），导致部署后的表现受限。

Method: 提出了一种结合分析物理先验与残差神经网络的条件感知动力学模型；利用循环编码器从交互数据中推断隐藏动力学状态；通过可微仿真进行策略训练；在部署时通过在线推理实现的条件来实时调整策略，而非直接调整模型残差。

Result: 在四旋翼无人机轨迹追踪实验中，该方法在应对循环扰动时的恢复速度比在线残差拟合方法快5倍，在大干扰情况下的悬停和追踪误差分别降低了65.7%和53.3%。

Conclusion: 通过在线推断隐藏的动力学条件，该框架实现了比传统在线适应方法更快的动态扰动恢复与更高的追踪精度，证实了在机器人控制中结合先验动力学模型进行连续学习的有效性。

Abstract: Robots deployed in the real world rarely operate under a single fixed dynamics model: wind changes, payloads vary, batteries drain, contacts shift, and hardware wears. Yet most learning-based controllers are trained once and deployed as if learning were complete. This prevents the robot from using deployment experience to further improve task performance. In this work, we propose a continual learning framework that uses real-world experience to improve robot policies under hidden and recurring dynamics. Our method learns a condition-aware dynamics model from real state-action trajectories by combining an analytical physics prior with a neural residual for unmodeled effects. A recurrent encoder infers the current hidden condition from recent interaction, and this estimate conditions both the residual model and the policy. Policy learning is performed via differentiable simulation using diverse learned dynamics sampled from the latent model. At deployment, these sampled conditions are replaced by conditions inferred online from recent real interaction, allowing the policy to recover recurring dynamics by recognition rather than residual re-fitting. Through extensive simulation studies and real-world experiments, we demonstrate that the framework improves policy performance under diverse unobserved disturbances. On real quadrotor trajectory tracking under changing wind, the policy recovers from recurring disturbances in roughly 1s, about 5x faster than online residual re-fitting. It also reduces large-disturbance hover and tracking errors by 65.7% and 53.3% over the state-of-the-art online adaptation approaches

</details>


### [262] [RouterVLA: Turning Smoke Tests into Supervision for Heterogeneous VLA Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27355)
*Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Ge Ma,Youran Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RouterVLA，通过合理重用机器人策略预部署测试阶段的“测试数据”，在保证评估客观性的前提下，实现策略调度性能的大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人团队在部署前通常会对候选的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略进行大规模的预评估测试，现有流程通常只提取最优策略。研究旨在探讨这些被放弃的测试数据是否可以被系统地重用，以训练更强大的策略选择器（Router），从而提升系统整体性能。

Method: 提出RouterVLA方法，采用“结果不相交交叉拟合”技术：利用历史测试记录（探针）为每个预训练的专家策略构建性能档案，并在与档案完全独立的测试中评估被选策略，以避免数据污染带来的性能评估偏差。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus数据集的34,752条记录上，使用所提方法将系统留一成功率从0.4686提升至0.6149（提升14.64个百分点）；研究发现直接复用评估数据会导致指标虚高1.87倍，强调了结果分离的必要性。

Conclusion: 通过“结果不相交交叉拟合”（outcome-disjoint cross-fitting）机制，可以在不引入额外偏差的情况下有效地重用预部署评估数据进行策略调度，从而显著提升系统性能。该研究证明了“调试/评估”（commissioning）过程本身蕴含了调度价值，而单纯增加评分模型的复杂度无法替代这一过程。

Abstract: We study whether pre-deployment evaluation rollouts can be reused to supervise policy selection. Robot teams routinely smoke test candidate vision-language-action (VLA) policies, then compress those trials into a global winner. RouterVLA evaluates this idea with outcome-disjoint cross-fitting: recorded probes build a profile for each frozen expert, and a separate trial scores the selected expert without entering its profile. Across 34,752 LIBERO-Plus rollout records, a transparent probe-success rule raises held-out success from 0.4686 to 0.6149, a +14.64pp gain. Under the scalar-only profiles studied here, learned scorers are statistically indistinguishable from this rule, showing that commissioning carries the routing value while extra scalar scorer capacity does not create it. Reusing the scored trial inflates the measured gain by $1.87\times$, so credible ledger routing needs outcome separation; model scaling improves individual policies, while commissioning-aware routing improves the system built from them.

</details>


### [263] [World Action Models Enable Continual Imitation Learning with Recurrent Generative Replays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27374)
*Manish Kumar Govind,Dominick Reilly,Smit Patel,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REGEN通过利用世界动作模型（WAMs）生成伪回放轨迹来进行持续模仿学习，有效缓解了遗忘问题，并消除了对存储原始演示数据的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人持续模仿学习中，如何避免灾难性遗忘的同时，无需存储海量原始人类演示数据（受存储限制或隐私制约），是一个亟待解决的问题。研究者希望利用世界模型（WAMs）的生成能力来解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出了REGEN（循环生成式回放）框架。该框架利用WAM递归地生成伪回放轨迹，并在无需存储原始人类演示数据的情况下，使机器人策略能够对先前学习的任务进行重演，从而应对持续模仿学习中的灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在仿真和现实任务操纵实验中，REGEN相比顺序微调将灾难性遗忘减少了高达50%，且性能接近于需要访问真实数据的特权经验回放方法。

Conclusion: 世界动作模型（WAMs）是实现无需存储演示数据的持续机器人学习的有前途的基础。研究指出了长序列视觉退化和动作-观测不一致是当前生成式回放的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Going beyond predicting robot actions, World Action Models (WAMs) can also generate future visual observations. We build on this generative capability to propose Recurrent Generative Replay (REGEN), a continual imitation learning framework that synthesizes pseudo-replay trajectories, enabling a robot policy to rehearse previously learned tasks without storing their original human demonstrations. During continual adaptation, REGEN recursively queries the WAM to synthesize pseudo-replay trajectories conditioned only on prior task instructions and current-task observations. Experiments in both simulation and real-world manipulation settings show that REGEN reduces catastrophic forgetting by up to $50\%$ relative to sequential fine-tuning, while approaching the performance of privileged experience replay methods that require access to real replay data. Finally, we analyze the factors limiting generated replay, identifying long-horizon visual degradation and action-observation inconsistency as the primary bottlenecks. Our results establish WAMs as a promising foundation for continual robot learning without stored demonstrations.

</details>


### [264] [Scalable Behavior Cloning with Open Data, Training, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27375)
*Arthur Allshire,Himanshu Gaurav Singh,Ritvik Singh,Adam Rashid,Hongsuk Choi,David McAllister,Justin Yu,Yiyuan Chen,Huang Huang,Pieter Abbeel,Xi Chen,Rocky Duan,Phillip Isola,Jitendra Malik,Fred Shentu,Guanya Shi,Philipp Wu,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ABC是一个开源的行为克隆堆栈，包含史上最大的130K片段开源操作数据集及全套软硬件方案，旨在通过提供可复现的工具链来加速机器人学习研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人操作研究缺乏大规模、可复现的开源数据集及基础设施，导致研究门槛高、实验结果难以横向对比。

Method: 构建了ABC-130K大规模机器人操作数据集（真实世界+仿真数据），开源了硬件设计、训练基础设施与仿真流程，并对比评估了DiT和VLA模型架构在行为克隆任务中的表现。

Result: 发布了包含130K个片段、3500小时操作数据的开源数据集；证明了仿真数据集可作为真实世界训练的可靠代理（Proxy）；实现了在复杂灵巧操作任务（如拆卸钱包内银行卡、折叠纸盒）中的成功应用。

Conclusion: ABC通过提供完整的开源工具链和大规模数据集，降低了行为克隆研究的门槛，并验证了仿真与真实世界训练的协同效应，为社区提供了统一的评估基准。

Abstract: We introduce ABC, a fully open-source stack for manipulation with behavior cloning. At its core is ABC-130K: the largest open-source teleoperation dataset to date, featuring 3,500 hours of data spanning over 130K episodes across 195 diverse tasks. Furthermore, we open-source our accessible hardware setup, training infrastructure, and simulation pipeline. We also release 400 hours of sim-teleop data and provide a co-training recipe that produces correlated simulation and real-world evaluation, offering a reliable proxy for ablating model-design and training decisions before costly real-world evaluation. We explore various training recipes and compare common architectural choices for Diffusion Transformers (DiT) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, grounding our findings in real-world evaluations. The resulting policies successfully execute dexterous tasks such as box folding and extracting credit cards from wallets. By providing a reproducible toolkit, we aim to place researchers on an equal footing, establishing the necessary foundation to learn the ABCs of Behavior Cloning together as a community.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] [Detecting and Controlling Sycophancy with Cascading Linear Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26155)
*Maty Bohacek,Rishub Jain,Nicholas Dufour,Thomas Leung,Chris Bregler,Roma Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用级联样本提取特征的新方法，通过捕捉与模型行为线性相关的细粒度特征，更有效地实现了对大模型（如降低谄媚倾向）的可解释性探测与精确控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的激活干预方法高度依赖成对的对比数据，且难以精确提取导致特定模型行为（如谄媚行为，sycophancy）的根本特征。亟需一种更有效的方法来提高特征提取的可靠性、解耦能力及干预的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了一种迭代式数据生成流程。该方法不依赖简单的二元对比样本，而是利用表现出与行为线性相关的“级联样本”（cascading samples）来剥离级联线性特征，从而实现特征的更好解耦，并以此进行模型激活干预（activation steering）。

Result: 研究人员成功利用该方法定位并抑制了模型中的“谄媚”特征。实验表明，这些特征构成了线性可分的子空间，在探测、确证性打分及干预任务中，该方法的效果优于或持平现有的LLM裁判及提示词基准，且计算成本更低。

Conclusion: 通过级联样本（cascading samples）提取的线性特征能够有效且鲁棒地对大模型（LLM）进行干预。该方法在减少计算开销的同时，提供了比传统“LLM-as-a-judge”或系统提示词更优的性能与可解释性保证，为模型对齐提供了新路径。

Abstract: Interpreting and controlling model behaviors through activation steering methods requires many pairs of contrastive samples that clearly exhibit desired or undesired behavior. These data pairs determine the degree to which interpretability frameworks can reliably detect model features responsible for a behavior, and therefore the ability to steer models toward or away from such behavior. In this work, we present an iterative data generation pipeline that isolates cascading linear features responsible for a behavior. Specifically, we show how moving beyond simple binary pairs of samples, and instead isolating samples that show degrees of features that scale linearly with behavior, allows for better disentanglement of features. We focus on detecting and steering away from sycophancy -- the tendency of language models to prioritize user validation. We demonstrate that sycophancy features discovered through cascading samples form linearly separable subspaces, and allow for selection of model activations that more clearly correspond to the desired behavior than baseline approaches. We also evaluate their ability to enable detection, deterministic scoring, and robust steering, and see that they either match or outperform LLM-as-a-judge and system prompting baselines while providing lower computational demand and more interpretability guarantees. Code & Data: https://cascading-feats.github.io/

</details>


### [266] [Life After Benchmark Saturation: A Case Study of CORE-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26158)
*Nitya Nadgir,Sayash Kapoor,Kangheng Liu,Peter Kirgis,Matilda Orona,Stephan Rabanser,Tilman Bayer,Abhishek Shetty,Yue Ling,Derrick Chan-Sew,Rumi Nakagawa,Saiteja Utpala,Zachary S. Siegel,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有的 AI 评估模式过于依赖“准确率”，本文主张通过多维度评估（效率、可靠性、人机协作等）来超越准确率饱和的限制，并以 CORE-Bench 为例，证明了多维度评估能提供更深入的性能洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的 AI 基准测试评估过于依赖“准确率”指标，当准确率饱和时基准测试往往被弃用，这导致研究者忽视了智能体在其他关键性能维度上的表现，限制了对智能体能力的全面理解。

Method: 以 CORE-Bench Hard 为研究案例，突破单一准确率指标，提出引入六个额外评价维度：结构效度（如捷径学习）、分布外泛化能力（OOD）、效率、可靠性、模型与基架（scaffold）的相对权重，以及人机协作带来的提升（uplift）。此外，改进了基准测试集（CORE-Bench v1.1）并增加了 OOD 测试套件。

Result: 1. 在 CORE-Bench Hard 中发现了传统单一评估难以察觉的结构效度问题；2. 证明即便准确率饱和，CORE-Bench v1.1 在衡量效率、可靠性及组件贡献度方面仍具有显著价值；3. 人机协作实验显示，智能体辅助可将计算复现任务的效率提升约两倍。

Conclusion: 仅仅关注准确率（Accuracy）作为评价智能体性能的指标是不够的。通过在多个维度（如稳健性、效率、人类协作增益等）进行评估，即便在准确率饱和的情况下，仍能挖掘出关于智能体性能的深度洞察，从而建立更严谨的评估范式。

Abstract: When a benchmark's accuracy saturates, it is often retired and replaced with a more challenging version. We show that this approach privileges accuracy and misses the opportunity to study six other key dimensions of agent performance: construct validity issues such as shortcuts, out-of-distribution generalizability, efficiency, reliability, the relative importance of the model versus the scaffold, and uplift from human-agent collaboration. We use CORE-Bench Hard, a benchmark for computational reproducibility of scientific code, as a case study to demonstrate that measuring agents along these dimensions yields meaningful insights into agent performance even after accuracy saturates. First, we surface threats to construct validity in CORE-Bench Hard that are difficult to anticipate with less capable agents. We introduce an improved benchmark, CORE-Bench v1.1, and an out-of-distribution task suite, CORE-Bench OOD. Second, we find that despite accuracy saturation, CORE-Bench v1.1 remains useful for measuring efficiency, reliability, model performance, and scaffold performance. Finally, we conduct a small-scale randomized experiment to measure uplift from human-agent collaboration on real-world computational reproducibility tasks. We find a statistically significant speedup by about a factor of two -- likely underestimated due to one-fifth of human-only reproductions reaching the time limit before completing -- and describe various other findings. Together, our contributions present a more rigorous alternative to the dominant accuracy-centric evaluation paradigm.

</details>


### [267] [Refusal Lives Downstream of Persona in Chat Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26161)
*Viola Zhong,Qirui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究证明大模型拒绝行为受人格设定门控；通过操纵激活空间中的人格方向，可有效抑制模型拒绝，证实了拒绝是深层逻辑的表现，而非单一孤立的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究倾向于将指令微调模型中的“拒绝（Refusal）”与“人格（Persona）”视为独立的激活路径，而忽略了两者之间潜在的相互影响（交互作用）。

Method: 对Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型进行激活空间分析：1. 提取“合规人格方向”与“拒绝方向”；2. 通过激活干预（Intervention）与投影（Projection）技术，研究两个方向的相互作用；3. 通过观察特定层面的方向干预对模型拒绝率的影响，定位拒绝行为的发生机制。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct中，通过激活“合规人格方向”，模型的拒绝率从97%显著下降至2%；研究表明，拒绝行为主要在模型的深层阶段被门控（Gated），且可以通过干预特定深层的表现层来操纵模型的拒绝行为。

Conclusion: 拒绝行为并非孤立机制，而是严重依赖于模型的人格设定（Persona）。拒绝机制在模型表现（Expression）阶段受到人格方向的门控（Gating），且该过程主要发生于模型的深层（Late-layer）。

Abstract: Linear directions in activation space have been identified for both refusal and persona traits in instruction-tuned chat models, but the two have been studied as separate mechanisms. We show they interact: a compliant persona gates refusal. In Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we extract a compliant model-persona direction and a refusal direction and intervene on both. Compliant persona steering suppresses refusal -- in Llama, the refusal rate falls from 97% to 2%. Reintroducing the refusal direction partially restores refusal at late layers but not at early ones. Projecting out the persona direction in a late-layer window restores it to baseline; projecting out a random direction does not. Refusal is therefore gated at the late-layer expression stage, downstream of where it is computed. Treating refusal as a single isolated direction misses its dependence on persona.

</details>


### [268] [AlgoEvolve: LLM-driven Meta-evolution of Algorithmic Trading Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26173)
*Dhruv Sharma,Gautam Shroff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AlgoEvolve框架，利用LLM进行算法交易策略的演化生成，并通过元进化机制优化Prompt提示词，成功在动态复杂的市场环境中实现了自动化的策略迭代与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中于静态代码基准测试，缺乏在大规模、噪声大、非平稳且高度不连续的实际应用场景（如算法交易）中的验证，需探索LLM在这些复杂环境下的程序演进潜力。

Method: 提出AlgoEvolve框架：1. 使用LLM作为语义变异算子进行程序合成；2. 结合严格的协议评估交易策略；3. 引入“元进化”外层循环，自动迭代优化引导内层循环生成代码的Prompt，平衡探索与开发。

Result: 系统能够涌现出适应市场变动的策略逻辑（如自动切换交易规则）；元进化循环发现的搜索启发式策略在性能上超过了人工设计的指令，且显著降低了零交易故障率。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的语义进化范式在处理复杂、非平稳环境（如算法交易）下的程序自动生成方面是有效且可行的。通过元进化机制优化引导策略生成的提示词（Prompts），能够显著提升搜索效率与策略质量。

Abstract: Recent work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can act as semantic mutation operators for the evolutionary discovery of programs and proofs. Most current applications focus on static coding benchmarks. We extend this paradigm to algorithmic trading. This domain is uniquely challenging because it is noisy, non-stationary, and highly discontinuous. We present AlgoEvolve, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework that generates, evaluates, and iteratively improves executable trading strategies. These strategies are expressed as Python code and evaluated through a rigorous testing protocol. Across multiple experiments, the system exhibits emergent regime-adaptive strategy logic, including autonomous shifts in trading rules. We further introduce a meta-evolutionary outer loop that evolves the prompts guiding program synthesis in the inner loop. This outer loop discovers improved search heuristics. These heuristics balance exploration and exploitation while reducing zero-trade failures. They consistently outperform initial human-designed instructions. The results demonstrate that LLM-based semantic evolution provides a viable approach for continual program synthesis in complex environments.

</details>


### [269] [Agentic Analysis for Agentic Infrastructure: An LLM-Powered Pipeline for Comparative Governance of DAO and Corporate AI Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26203)
*Yutian Wang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个LLM驱动的治理话语对比分析框架，通过对比ERC-8004与Google A2A，揭示了不同AI代理互操作性标准在治理参与和社区结构上的异同，并为构建更公平的AI标准提供了实证依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理协议激增，规范其互操作性的治理结构仍缺乏实证研究，亟需探究制度设计如何影响技术治理的权力结构、专题优先权和社区动态。

Method: 构建一个基于LLM的对比分析流水线，结合自动化标注（LLM-assisted coding）、神经主题模型（neural topic modeling）和多层网络分析（multi-layer network analysis）对治理话语进行大规模分析。

Result: 通过对4,323条治理记录的研究对比（ERC-8004与Google A2A），量化了治理形式与参与结构之间的关系，揭示了不同治理模式下社区参与的不平等性和碎片化状态。

Conclusion: 治理形态虽影响关注焦点，但无论去中心化还是企业主导，均存在相似的参与不平等和社区碎片化问题；开放治理模型可能促进更大程度的主题趋同。研究表明LLM辅助方法可有效推动技术治理的实证研究。

Abstract: As AI agent protocols proliferate, the governance structures shaping their interoperability standards remain empirically underexamined. We introduce an LLM-powered comparative pipeline for large-scale governance discourse analysis, integrating automated annotation, neural topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to study socio-technical power structures at scale. We validate it on two contrasting standards for agent interoperability: ERC-8004 (permissionless, on-chain) and Google A2A (corporate-led). Analyzing 4,323 governance participation records, we combine LLM-assisted coding, topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to examine how institutional design shapes thematic priorities and community structure. We find that while governance form influences substantive focus, both regimes exhibit comparable levels of participation inequality and community fragmentation. Discourse alignment is denser in the permissionless setting, suggesting that open governance may foster greater thematic convergence despite decentralized participation. These findings illustrate how LLM-assisted methods can advance the empirical study of technology governance, with implications for designing more equitable agentic AI standards. All data and code are openly available.

</details>


### [270] [Knowledge-augmented Agentic AI for Mental Health Medication Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26205)
*Huizi Yu,Jian Liu,Wenkong Wang,Lingyao Li,Jiayan Zhou,Zhaoqian Xue,Xiang Li,Xinxin Lin,Zhiying Liang,Zhuoru Wu,Siyuan Ma,Xin Ma,Lizhou Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个结合FDA正式报告与大规模社交媒体（Reddit/WebMD）数据的知识图谱框架，有效融合了官方安全记录与患者真实用药经验，实现了对精神药物副作用信号的实时监控，且通过溯源技术保持了证据来源的清晰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决目前精神科药物安全性信息的不对称问题：监管数据（权威但抽象）与患者叙述（真实但未经医疗验证）之间存在严重割裂，缺乏有效的整合机制，容易导致患者过度焦虑或错误的用药决策。

Method: 构建了一个基于知识图谱的多智能体框架，集成了Reddit、WebMD数据和FDA不良事件记录；使用LLM进行实体识别（F1分数高达0.973），并采用ATC-N、ICD-10和MedDRA词汇映射确保数据溯源和分类标准化。

Result: 1. 患者社区数据的内部一致性远高于其与监管报告的一致性；2. 患者社区数据往往能比FDA记录提前数百天发现药物不良反应趋势；3. 开发的实体识别管道在药物及疾病识别任务上表现出色。

Conclusion: 通过源感知（Provenance-aware）知识图谱集成监管数据与患者叙述，可提供更具审计价值、更具临床背景的心理药物安全性信息，是改善患者用药决策的有效路径。

Abstract: Patients increasingly seek medication information online, yet safety knowledge for psychiatric drugs is split between regulatory adverse-event records, which are authoritative but abstract, and patient narratives, which are experience-near but unvalidated. Integrating them without conflating evidence and anecdote is especially consequential in psychiatry, where poorly contextualised information can amplify fear, nocebo responses, and non-adherence. Here we develop a provenance-aware, knowledge-graph-based multi-agent framework unifying 466,525 Reddit posts, 60,782 WebMD reviews, and twenty years of U.S. FDA Adverse Event Reporting System records for nine antidepressants. A large-language-model entity-recognition pipeline benchmarked against physician annotations reached highest F1 scores of 0.969 for medications and 0.973 for conditions. The two community platforms were far more concordant with each other (overlap up to a Jaccard similarity of 0.905) than with regulatory reports, indicating that patient-generated data form a partly independent safety signal. For sertraline, many adverse events appeared in community sources hundreds of days before the corresponding FDA date. A Neo4j knowledge graph grounded in ATC-N, ICD-10, and MedDRA vocabularies preserves provenance, keeping every claim traceable and regulatory facts distinct from patient experience. These results establish source-aware integration as a route to more auditable psychiatric medication information, with usefulness and patient benefit to be tested prospectively.

</details>


### [271] [Accelerating Skill Assessment in Chess: A Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26267)
*Tianyuan Zhou,Zhizheng Fu,Tianming Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对传统 Elo 评分系统响应滞后的问题，本文提出了 DD-Elo 模型，通过引入漂移扩散模型（DDM）整合逐回合数据，在保持向后兼容性的同时，显著提升了技能评估的实时性和响应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Elo 系统仅依赖比赛结果，存在响应滞后问题；且在评分系统中纳入逐步详细信息面临噪声大、状态空间庞大等挑战。

Method: 提出了一种受认知神经科学中漂移扩散模型（DDM）启发的“漂移扩散增强 Elo 评级系统”（DD-Elo），将逐回合（move-by-move）数据整合到评级调整中，并从数学上证明了其与传统 Elo 系统相比具有有界的偏差。

Result: 实验表明，DD-Elo 在适应技能变化方面优于传统 Elo 系统，能够更快速、更准确地反映选手的实时技能水平。

Conclusion: DD-Elo 提供了一种解释性强、响应迅速且与传统 Elo 系统向后兼容的国际象棋评分解决方案。

Abstract: Rating systems such as Elo serve as the gold standard for matchmaking in competitive chess. However, they inherently suffer from response lag due to their exclusive reliance on match outcomes, neglecting the granular quality of gameplay. Nevertheless, incorporating move-by-move information into rating adjustments presents a significant challenge given the substantial noise and the vastness of the game-state space. To address this, we propose the Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System (DD-Elo), a novel skill assessment framework inspired by the drift diffusion model (DDM) from cognitive neuroscience. By modeling skill expression as a decision-making process, our model integrates move-level data to capture rapid skill fluctuations. We provide a rigorous mathematical derivation proving that DD-Elo maintains a bounded deviation from the traditional Elo system, ensuring theoretical alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DD-Elo adapts to skill changes faster than Elo. Our findings suggest that DD-Elo offers an explainable, highly responsive, and backward-compatible solution for chess rating ecosystems. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aquila-zhou1/DD-Elo .

</details>


### [272] [Governing Actions, Not Agents: Institutional Attestation as a Governance Model for Autonomous AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26298)
*Jakob Salfeld-Nebgen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对自主AI代理的治理模型：通过将代理的“推理规划”与“执行权限”分离，依托独立验证和密码学手段，强制高风险操作在满足预设先行条件后方可执行，从而实现安全、可审计的AI自主治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对AI代理在临床药物处方和软件部署等高风险场景中可能执行不可逆、后果严重的操作，现有的监控推理方法不足以防范风险。借鉴人类社会对独立实体的治理模式，需构建一种更严谨的执行限制机制。

Method: 提出了一种计算治理模型，将执行权限从AI代理的推理引擎中剥离。核心机制包括：1. 独立证明（由独立权威源验证先行条件）；2. 密码绑定（将证明与意图绑定）；3. 确定性执行策略评估；4. 防篡改日志记录与后续审计。

Result: 通过概念验证（Proof-of-Concept）实现，展示了该模型在软件部署和临床处方场景中的有效性，证明了在保留AI代理规划与推理能力的同时，能有效控制其高风险操作的执行能力。

Conclusion: 该计算治理模型通过将决策与执行分离，利用密码学证明和确定性策略，为自主AI代理执行高风险任务提供了一种更安全、合规的治理框架，有望减少AI自主决策带来的不可逆风险。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents may begin to perform consequential, irreversible actions such as clinical prescribing and production software deployment. This paper observes that human institutions have governed powerful autonomous actors not by monitoring their reasoning but by requiring independently attested evidence at the point of consequential action. We formalise this institutional pattern as a computational governance model for AI agent systems. Under the proposed model, an agent retains full autonomy over planning and reasoning but holds no execution authority over designated high-risk actions. Execution is conditional on preconditions that are each independently attested by a separate authoritative source, cryptographically bound to a declared intent, and evaluated by a deterministic policy. Decisions are recorded in a tamper-evident log amenable to independent re-verification. We present a proof-of-concept implementation and illustrate the model with examples from software deployment and clinical prescribing.

</details>


### [273] [COrigami: An AI Pipeline for Co-Designing Flat-Foldable Visually Recognisable Origami](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26299)
*Tom Zahavy,Shaobo Hou,Thomas Tumiel,James Doran,Francesco Faccio,Xidong Feng,Alex Havrilla,Igor Khytryi,Chenglei Li,Lisa Schut,Vivek Veeriah,Arijan Abrashi,Michał Kosmulski,Robert J. Lang,Nick Robinson,Brandon Wong,Marcus Chiam,Gloria Fang,Satinder Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了“COrigami”，一个通过自然语言生成计算折纸折痕图案的AI流水线；它通过整合几何约束求解与审美驱动的强化学习，解决了在严苛物理限制下进行艺术创作的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在处理有可验证解的问题上表现出色，但在处理需要同时满足严苛几何约束（如计算折纸的平展折叠方程）与主观审美要求的艺术创作时仍面临很大挑战。

Method: 提出了一个名为“COrigami”的端到端AI流水线，包含五个关键阶段：从自然语言生成语义火柴人、计算基准包装、求解平展折叠折痕图案、塑造折痕图案，以及通过基于自动审美评价循环的强化学习进行模型细化。

Result: COrigami系统能够从自然语言描述中生成符合数学严谨性的折纸图案，成功作为协作助手为人类艺术家提供结构化的设计起点。

Conclusion: 该研究成功整合了算法优化与审美评价循环，验证了AI系统能够在满足严格物理几何约束的前提下，实现具有数学基础的创造性艺术设计，为计算折纸领域提供了一个高效的辅助创作工具。

Abstract: While generative AI has achieved remarkable success in solving problems with verifiable solutions, generating physical art that satisfies both strict geometric constraints and subjective visual aesthetics remains a challenge. This paper presents an approach to tackle these difficulties in the domain of computational origami, a mathematically rigid environment that grounds artistic design within the equations of flat foldability. We present COrigami, an end-to-end AI-driven pipeline that assists the design cycle by generating crease patterns from natural language. Our pipeline involves generating a semantic stick figure, computing a base packing, solving for a flat-foldable crease pattern, shaping the flat-folded crease pattern, and refining the generated model using reinforcement learning driven by an autonomous aesthetic evaluation loop. Our system acts as a highly effective collaborative assistant, generating structural starting points that human artists can further expand and shape. By integrating algorithmic optimisation with autonomous aesthetic critique, this work demonstrates how AI systems can satisfy multi-objective physical constraints to enable reliable, mathematically grounded co-creativity.

</details>


### [274] [The Verification Horizon: No Silver Bullet for Coding Agent Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26300)
*Binghai Wang,Chenlong Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Jiajun Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Mouxiang Chen,Rongyao Fang,Siyuan Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuheng Jing,Zeyao Ma,Zeyu Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 随着编码智能体生成能力的增强，验证成为了主要瓶颈。本文指出验证存在意图模糊和优化偏差两大挑战，通过分析四种验证机制，提出验证器必须与生成模型共同演化，并强调要在可扩展性、忠实度和稳健性之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基础模型推理能力提升，代码生成已不再是瓶颈，“可靠验证”成为新挑战。现有的验证机制存在意图定义的不可完备性及训练中的奖励欺骗（Reward Hacking）问题。

Method: 提出从可扩展性（Scalability）、忠实度（Faithfulness）和稳健性（Robustness）三个维度衡量验证信号质量，并对比研究了四种奖励机制：测试验证器（代码）、准则验证器（前端任务）、人工验证（真实任务）及自动智能体验证器（长程任务）。

Result: 针对性的验证设计能有效抑制奖励欺骗，提升任务完成质量，并在多个内部及公开基准测试中显著提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 单一的固定奖励函数无法随生成模型能力的增长而持续有效；验证机制必须与生成器协同进化。实现可扩展性、忠实度和稳健性是构建有效验证器的核心挑战。

Abstract: A classical intuition holds that verifying a solution is easier than producing one. For today's coding agents, this intuition is being inverted: as foundation models develop stronger reasoning capabilities and engineering harnesses grow more sophisticated, generating complex candidate solutions is no longer difficult -- reliably verifying them has become the harder problem. Every verifier we can build is only a proxy for human intent, never the intent itself. This makes verification subject to a twofold difficulty: first, intent is underspecified by nature, making it inherently hard to faithfully check whether it has been fulfilled; second, during model training, optimization widens the gap between proxy and intent -- manifesting as reward hacking or signal saturation. To address this, we characterize the quality of verification signals along three dimensions -- scalability, faithfulness, and robustness -- and argue that achieving all three simultaneously is the central challenge. We further study four reward constructions: a test verifier for general coding tasks, a rubric verifier for frontend tasks, the user as verifier for real-world agent tasks, and an automated agent verifier for long-horizon tasks. Across different task types and policy capability levels, we conduct in-depth analysis and experiments on the core challenges of reward design and how to more effectively leverage reward signals. Experiments show that targeted verification design can effectively suppress reward hacking, improve task completion quality, and achieve significant gains across multiple internal and public benchmarks. These experiences collectively point to a core observation: no fixed reward function can remain effective as policy capability continues to grow; and verification must co-evolve with the generator.

</details>


### [275] [How Do Tool-Augmented LLM Agents Perform on Real-World Energy Analytics Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26346)
*David Akinpelu,Akintonde Abbas,Rereloluwa Alimi,Ayodeji Lana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对能源领域缺乏动态智能体评估的问题，提出了包含243个行业真实任务的基准测试，通过整合实时市场数据及专业工具，分析并评估了各类LLM在处理复杂能源市场分析与决策任务中的效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源领域缺乏针对智能体（Agent）的动态评估，现有的基准测试多停留在静态知识测评层面，无法满足能源行业对实时数据检索、复杂法规理解及多步定量推理的实际需求。

Method: 构建了一个包含243个专家级问题的能源市场分析基准，涵盖市场数据提取、知识解读及定量建模决策；为智能体配备了实时电力市场API、法规搜索、资费数据库及资产优化模型等工具；采用多维度评估协议（正确性、准确性、属性对齐、来源有效性）对闭源与开源模型进行综合评测。

Result: 通过对开源与闭源模型在配置专用工具后的表现进行定量分析，研究展示了模型能力在处理高频、高复杂度的能源分析任务中的差异，并验证了工具增强框架在提升智能体解决复杂行业问题中的关键作用。

Conclusion: 现有评估方案在能源领域多局限于静态知识回忆，本文通过引入真实世界的能源市场分析基准，揭示了LLM智能体在处理高风险专业领域复杂决策任务时的能力边界，并强调了领域专用工具与LLM结合的重要性。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks have emerged across general-purpose and domain-specific settings, including finance, coding, law, and drug discovery, yet energy-domain evaluations remain largely limited to static knowledge recall. This is a critical gap for a sector that requires live data retrieval, specialized regulatory and market knowledge, and multi-step quantitative reasoning under real-world constraints.
  We present an empirical study of tool-augmented LLM agents on real-world energy market analytics tasks. Our evaluation environment includes 243 expert-curated problems across three categories: (1) Market Data Retrieval and Analysis, (2) Knowledge Retrieval and Interpretation, and (3) Advanced Quantitative Modeling and Decision Analytics. Tasks include price and demand analysis, tariff impact modeling, asset revenue and returns estimation, hedging strategy analysis, and optimization modeling, with problems spanning multiple difficulty levels.
  Agents are equipped with a configurable suite of domain tools, including live electricity market APIs for major U.S. ISOs, regulatory docket search, utility tariff databases, asset optimization models, and retrieval-augmented generation over energy market documents. We assess agent responses using a multi-dimensional evaluation protocol that scores approach correctness, answer accuracy, attribute alignment, and source validity, with category-aware routing to match scoring criteria to question type. We evaluate both closed-source and open-source LLMs, providing a comparative analysis of how model capability and domain tooling interact in a high-stakes professional domain. Key artifacts are publicly released to support reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [276] [Instruction Bleed: Cross-Module Interference in Prompt-Composed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26356)
*Ching-Yu Lin,Yifan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“组合行为泄露”（CBL）概念，揭示了大语言模型在处理组合提示词时，因注意力机制缺乏物理隔离而产生的隐性模块间干扰。这种干扰虽然在单次QA测试中难以察觉，但在大规模部署中会产生系统性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示词编写的智能体系统中存在一种反复出现的故障模式：修改一个提示词模块会隐蔽地改变其他模块的行为，尽管它们之间没有共享变量或依赖关系。作者旨在形式化并研究这种“组合行为泄露”（CBL）现象。

Method: 定义了CBL概念，并设计了一个三通道实验协议（针对音量、内容、形式三个维度）在Claude Sonnet 3.5模型上进行了144次测试，并进行定量分析（Cohen's d）。

Result: 研究发现内容通道会产生显著的配对效应（Cohen's d = 0.63），尽管没有直接导致推荐结果翻转，但在大规模部署中，这种效应表现为模型行为的累积性微扰，且无法通过标准的QA测试发现。

Conclusion: 提出了“组合行为泄露”（CBL）这一系统级现象，认为这种跨模块干扰是提示词编写（prompt-composed）智能体评估中的必要指标。该研究揭示了即使在没有共享变量的情况下，大模型架构的注意力机制也无法提供模块间的逻辑隔离。

Abstract: Practitioners of prompt-composed agentic systems report a recurring failure mode: editing one prompt module silently shifts the behavior of others despite no shared variable or executable dependency. We formalize this as compositional behavioral leakage (CBL): interference between modules sharing a context window. CBL is enabled by architectural non-isolation: transformer self-attention provides no formal boundary between concatenated modules. We probe CBL on a deployed job-evaluation agent (Claude Sonnet 4.6, 144 trials) through a reusable three-channel protocol that perturbs non-focal modules along volume, content, and form. Only the content channel produces a detectable paired effect (Cohen's d = 0.63, bootstrap 95% CI excluding zero); no recommendation flipped -- a sub-threshold regime invisible to standard QA but compounding across the thousands of decisions a deployed agent makes. CBL is orthogonal to known agent-failure axes (adversarial injection, cognitive degradation, multi-agent fault propagation, privacy leakage). We contribute an operational definition, a reusable protocol, a falsifiable prediction set, and a system-class characterization, establishing cross-module interference measurement as a requirement for prompt-composed agent evaluation.

</details>


### [277] [What We are Missing in Multimodal LLM Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26348)
*Po-han Li,Shenghui Chen,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有MLLM评估基准局限于独立任务，缺乏对跨模态感知、时空理解及物理逻辑等深层能力的评估，限制了对模型真实潜力的准确判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）发展迅速，但现有的测试基准多局限于离散任务，无法有效衡量模型深度整合多模态信息及理解复杂现实世界的能力。

Method: 通过梳理和归类现有的多模态评估基准方法，识别出当前领域在评估维度上的缺失与不足，进而指出评估范式改进的必要性。

Result: 研究发现主流评估手段忽视了时空相干性、物理规律认知、模态间一致性及关键信息筛选等核心维度的考量。

Conclusion: 当前针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的评估基准存在显著局限，未能充分反映模型跨模态信息整合能力的真实水平；亟需开发能够涵盖时空相干性、物理世界理解、多模态一致性及选择性注意力的综合性评估框架。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can process diverse inputs, e.g., text, images, audio, and video, and generate textual responses. While their capabilities have advanced rapidly, evaluation of such models has not kept pace. Most existing evaluation benchmarks are limited to isolated tasks and reveal little about whether a model integrates information across modalities. We examine current means for evaluating MLLMs and review the existing benchmark taxonomy to identify gaps, including temporal-spatial coherence, physical world understanding, multimodal consistency, and selective attention. Addressing these gaps is essential for measuring real progress in multimodal intelligence and exposing capability boundaries.

</details>


### [278] [OpenFinGym: A Verifiable Multi-Task Gym Environment for Evaluating Quant Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26350)
*Kaicheng Zhang,Wen Ge,Lei Jiang,Weixin Yang,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Jialin Yu,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenFinGym 是一个统一的量化金融智能体开发环境，通过集成全流程任务评估、自动化流水线和严谨的执行框架，解决了现有金融 AI 评测单一、脱离实战的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型（LLM）金融 Agent 评测多集中于单一、孤立的任务，忽略了真实金融工作流的复杂性与多阶段依赖性，导致模型能力被高估，且缺乏在真实市场环境下的泛化能力与决策评估。

Method: 构建了一个统一的量化金融 GYM 环境（OpenFinGym），包含自动化任务构建流水线（将论文转化为可执行包）、容器化运行环境、低延迟模拟交易引擎、支持长周期预测，并集成了监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）训练接口。

Result: 建立了一个涵盖预测、市场生成、实时交易、欺诈检测等全流程的集成平台，支持防止数据泄露的验证机制，实现了从研究到执行的无缝闭环。

Conclusion: OpenFinGym 解决了现有量化金融 AI 评估碎片化的痛点，通过提供统一的、跨任务的自动化执行环境，为开发具有通用决策能力的金融 Agent 提供了基石。

Abstract: Although large language model agents are increasingly applied to quantitative-finance workflows, their evaluation remains fragmented across isolated tasks, while the financial relevance of benchmark tasks is often overlooked. Yet financial workflows are inherently multi-stage, spanning interdependent tasks such as forecasting, strategy construction, risk management, and trading. Existing platforms typically focus on a single task, and can therefore overstate agent competence and fail to reveal weaknesses in generalization, real-market interaction, and financially meaningful decision-making. We introduce OpenFinGym, a unified gym environment for quantitative-finance agent development that covers forecasting, market generation, real-time trading, and fraud detection under a single execution and verification interface. OpenFinGym additionally provides an automated task-construction pipeline that turns quantitative finance publications into executable task packages; a containerised runtime with a host-side verifier service that supports scalable agent rollouts and prevents runtime train-test leakage; a paper trading engine with a low-latency data-stream design; deferred-resolution support for long-horizon and event-market forecasts; and integration for SFT and RL post-training

</details>


### [279] [Accelerating Returns and the Qualitative Engine for Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26359)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 技术加速定律虽能解释执行能力的提升，但无法解决科学发现中关键的定性推理瓶颈，因此作者提出了“科学定性引擎”（QES）来保留并增强人类特有的科学发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前对AI技术指数级发展的过度乐观，指出技术累积并不等同于科学发现能力的提升；旨在探讨如何弥补当前AI在概念创新和结构性推理方面的短板。

Method: 通过对Kurzweil“加速回报定律”进行数学解释，对比当前 frontier AI 与人类在 ARC-AGI-3 基准测试中的差距，论证技术执行能力的提升与定性科学推理能力之间的鸿沟，并提出“科学定性引擎”（QES）作为解决方案框架。

Result: 论证了AI在处理定性、框架转换性质的科学问题时远落后于人类。当前大语言模型等技术虽能加速定量执行，但未能解决科学发现中关于“何时及如何进行范式转移”的核心难题。

Conclusion: 即使技术加速（如Kurzweil所言）在执行层面成立，它也无法解决科学发现的核心瓶颈——定性推理能力。科学发现的本质是人类智慧的一种形式，值得被保留和结构化。QES（科学定性引擎）旨在补充这一缺失的推理能力，使科学发现的过程更具可理解性和传承性。

Abstract: Ray Kurzweil described a thesis of accelerating returns, which is the most influential narratives in discussions of technological progress. Its central claim is that advances in multiple technological fields, especially compute, artificial intelligence, brain science, and biotechnology, interact in such a way that progress becomes self-amplifying and approximately exponential. This paper gives a simple mathematical interpretation of that claim and then argues that, even if such acceleration is real, it does not by itself resolve the central problem of scientific discovery. The reason is that accelerating returns apply most naturally to executional and infrastructural capability, whereas genuine discovery often depends on a different capacity: qualitative reasoning about when a current framework is structurally inadequate and what conceptual move is needed next. Recent ARC-AGI-3 results sharpen this distinction: humans solve the benchmark at ceiling, whereas frontier AI systems remain below 1%, indicating that the gap between current AI and human flexible reasoning is still very large. At the same time, Demis Hassabis has emphasized that humans must retain their sense of meaning and what they choose to focus their lives on, a reminder that the future of AI is not only a technical forecast but also a question of what forms of human understanding are worth preserving and transmitting. This paper positions the Qualitative Engine for Science (QES) [3] as a response to that missing capacity. In this view, the Kurzweil theory helps explain why quantitative capability may accelerate, while QES addresses the central problem in scientific discovery that acceleration alone does not solve. Its value does not depend on when AGI arrives, but on the fact that the processes of scientific discovery themselves constitute a form of human wisdom worth preserving, organizing, and making accessible.

</details>


### [280] [Narration-of-Thought: Inference-Time Scaffolding for Defeasible Ethical Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26366)
*Patrick Cooper,Alvaro Velasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NoT 是一种不需要额外训练的结构化提示词方法，通过强制模型在道德决策前梳理多方利益与不确定性，有效解决了思维链在复杂道德任务中的逻辑缺陷，提升了决策的鲁棒性与可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准思维链（CoT）在处理道德困境时存在“利益相关者崩溃”（忽视多方立场）和“不确定性抑制”（草率决策）两大失效模式，影响模型决策的科学性与透明度。

Method: 提出 Narration-of-Thought (NoT) 系统提示词，将思维链结构化为五个部分：主角、利益相关者、两步后果、不确定性及最终承诺。同时引入五轮多利益相关者辩论协议及文本梯度下降优化。

Result: 实验表明 NoT 显著降低了失效比例（利益相关者崩溃从 31% 降至 1% 以下，不确定性抑制最高降低了 71%），并在多方辩论协议中将困境下的共识达成率提升至 95%-100%。

Conclusion: NoT 框架有效解决了大模型处理道德困境时的 stakeholder collapse 和 uncertainty suppression 问题，不仅通过将思维链结构化显著增强了模型的推理透明度，还为安全、可审计的代理部署提供了一个可靠的框架。

Abstract: Standard chain-of-thought on moral dilemmas exhibits two failure modes: stakeholder collapse (the trace names at most one party with a stake in the outcome) and uncertainty suppression (no explicit unknowns or hedges before committing to an action). We introduce narration-of-thought (NoT), a system prompt that structures chain-of-thought into five sections: protagonist, stakeholders, two-step consequences, uncertainty, then commitment. NoT adds no training, parameters, or fine-tuning. On 100 DailyDilemmas scenarios across four generators from three vendors, NoT cuts stakeholder collapse from up to 31% to under 1% and uncertainty suppression from up to 72% to 1-24% on every model. A matched-budget verbose-CoT control rules out token spend as the active ingredient; NoT retains Cliff's delta advantages of +0.79 to +0.90 on stakeholder count and +0.65 to +0.93 on uncertainty score for three of four generators, and a section ablation attributes each shift to its specific sub-instruction. Textual-gradient descent initialised at NoT improves the scaffold further; a cross-family training judge (different vendor from the generator) dominates an in-family one on every measured axis. Extended to a five-round multi-stakeholder debate protocol, the scaffold converts a 6% standoff into 95% full consensus on a calibration set and 100% combined convergence on a DailyDilemmas replication. The resulting traces externalise the stakeholders, consequences, and uncertainty grounding each commitment, providing an auditable substrate for dependable agentic deployment.

</details>


### [281] [Geometry-Aware MCTS for Extremal Problems in Combinatorial Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26399)
*Luoning Zhang,Xu Zhuang,Tianhao Wang,Nathan Kaplan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对组合几何中的约束优化问题，本文提出了一种几何感知 MCTS 框架，通过引入增量更新、对称性约束和剪枝技术，有效规避了传统算法的组合爆炸和学习模型的训练瓶颈，在多个经典难题上创下了新的计算记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决组合几何中具有严格全局约束的极致点集配置问题。传统精确求解器面临组合爆炸，而通用的RL和Transformer模型则受限于稀疏奖励（“有效性悬崖”）和二次方 Token 消耗限制。

Method: 提出了一个几何感知蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）框架。核心机制包括：1. 增量式更新可行动作空间，将几何约束检查复杂度从 O(n³) 降低至 O(n²)；2. 利用几何对称性进行节点展开时的规范化剪枝（canonical pruning）；3. 采用对称批处理转换（symmetric batch transitions）加速搜索过程。

Result: 在六个测试问题中的五个上创造了新的最优计算结果。在 Max-N3IL 问题上，针对 82 ≤ n ≤ 119 的网格找到了约 1.8n 大小的配置；在 Smallest Complete Set 问题上找到了约 0.95n 大下的配置，提供了新的上界。

Conclusion: Geometry-Aware MCTS 是一种高效的框架，显著优于传统求解器和神经网络模型，能够解决组合几何中的极致问题，并为多个经典问题设定了新的计算基准。

Abstract: We study certain extremal problems in combinatorial geometry that ask about configurations of points in an $n \times n$ grid that satisfy strict, global geometric constraints. Classical exact solvers suffer from combinatorial explosion for these types of problems, and standard reinforcement learning and transformer-based models struggle with the sparse reward "validity cliff" and quadratic token-consumption limits. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose a Geometry-Aware Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) framework. Our approach strictly enforces geometric constraints through incremental updates to the feasible action space. For constraints about collections of collinear points, like those that occur in the classic No-Three-in-Line problem (Max-N3IL), this mechanism reduces the constraint checking complexity from $O(n^3)$ to $O(n^2)$. To improve search efficiency, we exploit geometric symmetries in two ways: canonical pruning during node expansion to reduce the branching factor, and symmetric batch transitions to accelerate the discovery of promising configurations. We perform extensive experiments and establish new best-known computational results on five out of six of the problems that we considered. Notably, for Max-N3IL we find configurations of size roughly $1.8 n$ for grids of size $82 \le n \le 119$. For the Smallest Complete Set problem, we find configurations of size roughly $0.95 n$, providing new upper bounds within the tested grids. This work establishes Geometry-Aware MCTS as a highly adaptable framework for discovering novel configurations in combinatorial geometry.

</details>


### [282] [When Agents Meet Electric Bus Fleet Operations: Pricing Behavior, Trade-offs, and Policy Implications in an Aggregator Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26400)
*Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Ali Eslami,Luis Miranda-Moreno,Jiangbo Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种智能体聚合器框架，用于协同优化电动公交车队的调度与V2G运营。研究表明，该系统虽能提升运营灵活性与自适应性，但必须配套透明的监管与定价规则，以防止过度的利益倾轧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动公交车队运营面临复杂的多变量环境（包括服务可靠性、电池状态、充电桩可用性、电价波动及V2G机会），传统模型难以应对动态扰动。需引入智能体系统来实现数据集成与自动化协调。

Method: 提出一种“智能体聚合器框架”。该框架由两部分组成：1. 核心优化调度模型：基于物理约束（车辆、充电桩、电池、V2G电网交换）执行调度；2. 智能体层：包含干扰检测、费率适应和调度评估智能体，负责实时监测环境变化并触发重优化。

Result: 该框架能有效维护调度可行性，实现电网与车队的自适应协调，提升充电和V2G的灵活性利用效率。研究发现存在关键权衡：以利润为导向的智能体配置在降低复杂性的同时，可能会从PTO手中抽取利益。

Conclusion: 智能体聚合器在管理电动公交车V2G运营方面具有巨大潜力，但在公共车队场景中应用时，必须建立透明的协调模式、可审计的费率设定机制以及明确的价值共享规则，以避免利润导向的调控损害公共交通运营商（PTO）的利益。

Abstract: Agentic systems are changing how complex operational tasks are coordinated, introducing a new paradigm for connecting heterogeneous data sources and automating processes. Electric bus fleets provide a relevant test case. Their operation requires continuous coordination between service reliability, battery state-of-charge, charger availability, electricity prices, route-energy uncertainty, and vehicle-to-grid (V2G) opportunities. This paper proposes an agentic aggregator framework that streamlines this decision environment by coupling an optimization-based electric bus scheduling model with supervisory agents for disturbance detection, tariff adaptation, and schedule evaluation. The optimization core enforces physical feasibility across routes, chargers, batteries, and V2G exchanges, while the agentic layer interprets changing operating conditions, triggers real-time re-optimization when needed, and defines how flexibility value is allocated between the aggregator and the public transport operator (PTO). A realistic depot case study evaluates day-ahead and real-time operations under profit-based and operation-based coordination modes, considering service delays, route-energy deviations, electricity price shocks, and combined disturbances. The results show that agentic aggregation can support adaptive fleet-grid coordination by maintaining feasible schedules, activating re-optimization selectively, and improving the use of charging and V2G flexibility. However, they also reveal a critical trade-off: the same agentic capability that reduces operational complexity can extract value from the PTO when configured around profit-oriented pricing. These findings suggest that agentic aggregators can become useful for managing electric bus V2G operations, but their deployment in public-fleet contexts requires transparent coordination modes, auditable tariff-setting, and explicit value-sharing rules.

</details>


### [283] [Unbiased Canonical Set-Valued Oracles Via Lattice Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26418)
*Jobst Heitzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对AI预测影响预测结果的自指难题，本文提出了一种基于不动点定理构建“自洽信度集”的方法，取代单一概率预测，以保持结论的现实有效性和逻辑一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决非代理（non-agentic）预言机AI在预测未来事件时面临的“自指问题”：当AI的预测影响了预测对象的概率时，传统的反事实问询会使预测失去现实意义。

Method: 利用格论（lattice theory）和Knaster-Tarski不动点定理，在闭信度集的完备格上定义单调算子，并取其最小不动点，以构建规范的自洽预测区间。

Result: 证明了所构建的信度集存在性、自洽性和非空性；在非操作性问题中退化为经典点估计；在二元事件中，其规范答案表现为一个非平凡的概率区间。

Conclusion: 研究表明，使用Knaster-Tarski不动点定理可以为自指性预言机AI定义一个规范且非平凡的“信度集（credal set）”，该集合在因果干预下保持无偏且自洽，解决了传统反事实问询导致的无用性问题。

Abstract: A non-agentic "oracle" AI that estimates probabilities of future events faces a self-reference problem: once its answer is learned and acted upon, it can change the very probability it was asked to report. One response, advocated for the Scientist AI programme, is to ask only counterfactual questions, evaluated as if the answer had no influence. We observe that such answers tend to become irrelevant the moment they are learned, precisely because their premise is then false. We therefore explore a self-referential alternative in which the oracle reports not a single probability but a credal set that is simultaneously unbiased and self-consistent with the consequences of being learned. The naive self-consistency requirement is satisfied by too many sets (including the useless answer $[0,1]$), so the problem is to single out a canonical, nontrivial member. We do so with the Knaster--Tarski fixed-point theorem on the complete lattice of closed credal sets, taking the least fixed point of a suitably defined isotone operator; a variant instead reports the least fixed point that contains every self-consistent point estimate. We prove existence, self-consistency, and nonemptiness, show that the construction collapses to the classical point answer for non-performative questions, and that for a binary event the canonical answer is, under a natural hull-factoring assumption, an interval. The development is purely lattice-theoretic and extends unchanged from a binary event $B$ to an arbitrary random variable $X$, with $P(B\mid A,C)$ replaced by the conditional law $\mathcal{L}(X\mid A,C)$. We close with open questions, including whether the interval characterization itself survives that generalization.

</details>


### [284] [AgentX: Towards Agent-Driven Self-Iteration of Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26859)
*Changxin Lao,Fei Pan,Guozhuang Ma,Han Li,Huihuang Lin,Jijun Shi,Kangzhi Zhao,Kun Gai,Mo Zhou,Qinqin Zhou,Quan Chen,Ruochen Yang,Shifu Bie,Shuang Yang,Shuo Yang,Wenhao Li,Wentao Xie,Xiao Lv,Xuming Wang,Yijun Wang,Yiming Chen,Yusheng Huang,Zhongyuan Wang,Zibo Zhao,Zijie Zhuang,Baoning Xia,Chao Liu,Chaoyi Ma,Chubo He,Dawei Cong,Feng Jiang,Gang Wang,Guilin Xia,Hanwen Xu,Jiahong Xie,Jiahui Qiao,Jian Liang,Jiangfan Yue,Jing Wang,Jinghan Yang,Jinghui Jia,Kan Qin,Lei Wang,Ming Li,Peilin Song,Pengbo Xu,Qiang Luo,Ruiming Tang,Shiyang Liu,Shuxian Jin,Tao Wang,Tao Zhang,Xiang Gao,Xianghan Li,Yingsong Luo,Yiwen Ning,Yongcheng Liu,Yuan Guo,Zhaojie Liu,Zhenkai Cui*

Main category: cs.AI
